He has big ears.）*Are the frogs/birds kind?*（Yes!）*Why?*（They look for his family together.）
**Track 31 —— Bunny's family（录音文本）：** *Bunny is sad.  Bunny: Uh-oh!… Where's my family?  Bunny looks.  Bunny: Are you my family?  Mummy squirrel: No, sorry. Our ears are small. Your ears are big!  Let's look together.*  … *Bunny: Are you my family?  Daddy frog: No, sorry. Our mouths are big. Your mouth is small!  Let's look together.  Bunny: Are you my family?  Mummy bird: No, sorry.… There's your family!  Bunny is with his family AND his new friends.  Bunny is happy!*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍，配拟声与动作；或播放读写故事视频。指出熟悉词如 ears, nose, mouth, big, small, friend。指最后一幅 *Look! Bunny is happy.* 逗号：*This is Bunny's family and these are his friends.*
**Activity Book（p48）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 说 *Look! Bunny is sad. Where's Bunny's family?* 逐指小图问 *Is this Bunny's family?* 儿童只描兔子一家。
**Finishing the class：** *Why was Bunny sad? Who did he ask about his family? What happened in the end?* 表扬认真听。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 48–49

**Objective：** Recognise the value of being kind, from the story.
**Value：** **Be kind（友善待人）。**
**Skills：** Emotional Development: Empathy and relationship skills: Consoles others (e.g. by hugging them, offering them a toy).
**Story language：** bird, bunny（rabbit）, frog, squirrel, family, friend, ears, mouth, big, small, happy, sad.
**Materials：** soft toy rabbit or rabbit flashcard; Big Book; colouring pencils or crayons; photo of each child stuck on card; sticky tack; a large cut-out heart shape (preferably on coloured paper).
**Starting the class：** 出示玩具兔子或闪卡说 *Look, it's Bunny! Move your ears like Bunny.* 手放头上像耳朵，儿童模仿；*Move your nose like Bunny!* 抽动鼻子；*Move your mouth like Bunny!* 咀嚼状；以不同顺序重复，儿童听并做，看能否同时做到三件事。用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 一起看故事视频，暂停问 *Who has small ears? Who has a small mouth?*
**With the book *Listen.***： 展示 Big Book 让儿童指出图中动物善待 Bunny 的画面。问动物如何善待：儿童用 L1 说它们帮 Bunny 找到家人；问动物如何知道 Bunny 需要帮助（他看起来很伤心）。
**After the book：** 谈善待他人的重要性；请儿童举例自己何时对人友善（有人伤心时拥抱、与朋友分享玩具、因为有人显得孤单而邀请他玩等）。*IN the book*：指图中受伤的男孩，用英中问 *What's happened?*（He's fallen over.）说 *Look! This girl is being kind!* 儿童说她在做什么，改述成英语（*She is giving him a plaster.*）；引出还能怎样善待男孩（give him a hug, find his mummy, give him a sweet）。英语与 L1 描述日常情境问如何善待：*There's a new boy in our class*（Ask him to play.）*Your sister is feeling ill*（Make her laugh.）；儿童可结对演出来。
**Activity Book（p49）*Draw. Colour. Say.*** 儿童把自己画进图中，确保小男孩没事，然后涂色；涂后儿童指人物说 Boy/Girl，能力强可说如 *Kind girl.*
**Learning through play — A kindness wall：** 在墙中央贴大爱心写上 *Be kind!*；用魔术贴沿底部贴每位儿童的照片。解释每当有儿童做了一件友善的事，就有人把他的照片移进爱心中央；尤其在游戏时间后多鼓励。当你注意到有人照片在爱心里，问是谁、为什么搬进去，并表扬善举（如让该儿童抱抱软玩具或坐特殊坐垫）。
**At home：** 让儿童与家人谈自己何时对人友善。
**Finishing the class：** 用 L1 问 *What is our world like if we are all kind?* 播 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束，鼓励儿童合唱时互相微笑。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 50

**Objective：** Name more parts of the body and follow colouring instructions.
**Vocabulary：** arms, feet, hands, legs.
**Language：** **Colour the (arms).**
**Materials：** flashcards red, yellow, blue, brown (Unit 3) ; arms, feet, legs, hands; puppet; Unit 4 character story video; simple outlines of people on paper for colouring; yellow, brown, blue and red colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在墙不同位置展示 red, yellow, blue, brown 闪卡；播放 **The colours chant（Track 25）**儿童听到每个颜色跑到正确闪卡旁起舞，鼓励跟唱。
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa。在板上画简笔小人，指腿问 *What are these?* Pippa 答如 *Legs!* 儿童重复；用 arms, hands, feet 重复。Pippa 给全班指令，如 *Wave your arms! Move your legs! Show me your hands. Stamp your feet!* 也可用闪卡教身体部位。播放角色故事视频，每场暂停指点角色问 *Who's this?* 用 L1 问 *What's happening?*
**With the book（PB p50）*Listen. Point. Trace.***： 解释或引出 Kim 和 Dan 在涂一幅图；问儿童喜不喜欢涂色。指每幅图中身体部位和 Kim/Dan 正用到的颜色。播音频儿童听；*Listen and point.* 再播儿童听指身体部位。*Say Trace.* 指轮廓解释儿童需描 Kim 和 Dan 每幅图所图身体部位的圆圈（1 arms, 2 feet, 3 legs, 4 hands）。
**Track 32（录音文本）：** *Mummy: OK! Colour the picture. Colour the arms yellow.  Dan: Ooh, yellow. I like yellow.  Mummy: Very good. Now, colour the feet brown. … Mummy: OK. Now colour the legs.  Dan: What colour?  Mummy: Blue. Colour the legs blue. … Mummy: Now colour the hands red.  Kim: Red. Ooh, red! I like red!*
**After the book：** 在板上贴一些简单人形轮廓；每个轮廓叫一个儿童上前，确保有黄、棕、蓝、红蜡笔。发指令如 *Colour the arms red.* 儿童照做；换儿童给不同指令，如 *Colour the legs yellow.* 直到所有新部位都涂色。
**Activity Book（p50）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童描线完成图画；走动问 *What are these? Are these legs or arms?* 然后指不同身体部位全班一起说词（arms, hands, feet, legs）。
**Finishing the class：** 儿童起立，发指令如 *Wave your hands. Shake one leg. Touch your nose.* 重复、混合并加速；展示身体部位闪卡指图读词鼓励跟上，Pippa 指卡并给全班鼓掌（或你自己指并拍手）。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice with a chant）—— Pupil's Book page 51

**Objective：** Practise parts of the body and colours with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** arms, feet, hands, legs.
**Language：** **Colour the (arms).**
**Materials：** flashcards hands, arms, feet, legs; yellow, brown, red, blue (Unit 3) ; Unit 4 chant video; red, blue, yellow, brown, pink and green colouring pencils or crayons; simple outlines of robots on paper for colouring; puppet.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；做「Hokey Cokey」改编词，如 *Put one arm in. One arm out. In out! In out! Shake it all about!*；或混好身体部位闪卡叫一儿童上前挑一张，向全班展示说词，再（在你帮助下）给全班指令，如 *Wave one hand!*
**Before the book：** 展示颜色闪卡指读，儿童重复；请不同儿童指教室内各色物品（*Nacho, point to something yellow!*）。画一个机器人并模仿走路说话，说 *I'm a robot!* 儿童模仿并说词。
**With the book（PB p51）*Listen. Colour. Sing.***： 逐指机器人身体部位（arms, hands, legs, feet）问 *What are these?* 儿童答如 *Legs!* 确保每儿童有红、蓝、黄、棕蜡笔；*Listen and colour.* 播 **The robot chant**，儿童为每个部位选正确蜡笔（可用记号标注），需时暂停；查问 *What colour are the arms?* 播完儿童继续涂完（红 arms、棕 hands、黄 legs、蓝 feet）。完成后可再播 chant 让儿童跟上，指图中正确部位；或站起像机器人随节奏移动并指自己身体部位。
**Track 33 The robot chant：** *Look at the robot. Colour the robot. [x2] Look at the arms, arms, arms, arms. Colour the arms red. [x2] Look at the hands, hands, hands. Colour the hands brown. [x2] Look at the legs, legs, legs. Colour the legs yellow. [x2] Look at the feet, feet, feet. Colour the feet blue. [x2]*
**After the book：** 再播 The robot chant 教动作（arms：双臂像风中树枝摇摆；hands：像挥手说 hello；legs：逐条抖腿；feet：跺脚）；儿童跟动作、能力允许时跟词。在板上贴一些机器人简笔轮廓，每个叫一儿童上前，备好黄、棕、蓝、红蜡笔；鼓励全班发指令如 *Colour the arms red.* 儿童照做；换儿童让全班发不同指令如 *Colour the legs yellow.*
**Activity Book（p51）：** *Look. Colour. Say.* 指左边的女孩问 *What colour are the arms?*（Red!）指右边的女孩说 *Colour the arms red.* 儿童看两图并把身体部位涂成一致；边涂鼓励说如 *Colour the hands.* 完成后指出左边女孩身体部位，儿童指右边匹配部位说词，如 hands。
**Learning to Learn / Self-evaluation：** 当 Pippa 问 *Can you do it?* 时可让儿童用自我评估技巧（TB 第 7 页）表示对表现的感觉。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers 数字）—— Pupil's Book page 52

**Objective：** Practise recognising patterns（练习识别模式）.
**Vocabulary：** classroom objects, colours, parts of the body.
**Language：** **What are these? (Red) (hands).**
**Materials：** snap cubes or building bricks in known colours and different sizes; yellow, brown and blue colouring pencils or crayons; classroom objects or toys to make repeating patterns (e.g. small bears and big bears, crayons in different colours); soft toys, building blocks or snap cubes in different colours and sizes.
**Skills：** Critical Thinking: Understanding and analysing links between ideas: Sorts, arranges and describes objects by shape, size, colour, weight, texture and position.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；给儿童不同颜色和大小的软玩具、积木或 snap cubes让他们自由玩；边玩边用英语交谈，问颜色、观察他们做的模式。*Before the book*：用一排软玩具（如 big teddy, two small teddies, big teddy, two small teddies）或积木（red brick, blue brick, red brick, blue brick）做一个模式；若有儿童在自由玩时做出模式，展示给全班。*With the book*：指模式中的物品用升调说如 *Red, blue, red, blue…?* 儿童答 Red！*Yes!* 请一儿童完成模式下一样；用教室物品（book, crayon, book, crayon…）重复。
**With the book（PB p52）*Look. Match. Say.*** 播放 **The robot chant（Track 33）**儿童听跟上。与全班指第一行图一起说身体部位，儿童说出下一个并描线连到缺失的身体部位（mouth）。用第二、三行重复。连线完成后一起说模式（Eyes, mouth, eyes, mouth.）。儿童自己练说模式（Eyes, mouth, eyes, mouth. Hands, feet, hands, feet. Hands, eyes, hands, eyes.）
**After the book：** 用身体部位说模式，儿童听并指自己身体部位，如 *Eyes, nose, feet. Eyes, nose, feet. Eyes, nose, feet.*
**Learning through play — A pattern/fine motor skills game：** 用 snap cube 或积木示范模式（blue, brown, blue, brown, blue），给每个/每对儿童一个示范；提供充足积木和不同颜色，儿童复刻你的模式，鼓励用英语说；儿童也可自己创造模式给同伴复制。
**Activity Book（p52）：** *Look. Colour. Say.* 指第一行问 *What are these?*（Hands.）与全班说颜色（Red hands, yellow hands, red hands…?）儿童说 Yellow（hands）并涂轮廓；鼓励儿童推算其他行模式并涂色。涂后举书一起说（Red hands, yellow hands, red hands, yellow hands. Blue eyes, brown eyes, blue eyes, brown eyes. Red nose, blue nose, red nose, blue nose.）
**At home：** 让儿童在家里用玩具做模式给家人看；家长/看护人可拍照让儿童与全班分享。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束；确保一起收拾。

---

### Lesson 10（Social studies：Feelings）—— Pupil's Book page 53

**Objective：** Talk about feelings.
**Vocabulary：** Feelings（happy, sad）.
**Language：** **He's/She's (happy).**
**Materials：** puppet; Pippa's house; pictures of people who are happy or sad (optional); yellow and blue colouring pencils or crayons.
**Skills：** Emotional Development: Identifying and understanding emotions: Identifies core emotional states (e.g. happy, sad, excited, bored, angry, scared or hurt).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The body song（Track 27）**儿童跟上做动作；'If you're happy…' 段落务必微笑并显得十分开心（指你微笑的嘴）。
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出 Pippa。微笑说 *I'm happy!* Pippa 也说 *I'm happy!* 展示不同面带欣喜的人或画在板上，Pippa 说 *Look! Happy! He's/She's happy!* 鼓励儿童微笑说 happy。用同样步骤教 sad（皱眉说 *I'm sad.*，Pippa 说 *I'm sad.*，展示伤心的人）。把 happy、sad 的人图乱序展示在儿童可及高度，指不同图全班说 happy 或 sad；叫一志愿者上前说 happy，儿童指 happy 的图，混着 happy/sad 重复。
**With the book（PB p53）*Look. Listen. Colour.*** 在板上画笑脸和哭脸，指让儿童说 happy 或 sad。把其中一张照片贴到脸上方，儿童说 happy 或 sad 给脸涂对应表情色（几次可叫志愿者涂）。*Listen. Colour.* 播音频暂停第一句后，儿童指照片下方正确的脸并涂色，用第二句重复。
**Track 34（录音文本）：** *She's sad.  He's happy.*
**After the book：** 让 Pippa 显得很伤心。说 *Look at Pippa! She's…* 儿童说 sad；一起说 *She's sad.* 再让 Pippa 开心，以同样方式练 *She's happy.* 叫志愿者（男孩女孩）上前向全班表演开心或伤心，儿童说如 *He's sad. / She's happy.*
**Activity Book（p53）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 儿童看色键与图，用黄色描开心儿童、蓝色描伤心儿童；描后指说 e.g. sad 或 She's sad.
**Finishing the class：** 再让 Pippa 显得伤心，儿童说 *She's sad.* 鼓励全班 *Aaaah!* 解释人人偶尔伤心，没关系。请儿童帮 Pippa 开心，提出不同方法（问 Pippa 怎么了、讲笑话、拥抱她）；让不同儿童试试，每次让 Pippa 开心并说 *Look! Now Pippa's happy! Well done!* 用 L1 问 *What did you learn today? Why is it important to work out how other people are feeling?* 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 54

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** colours, parts of the body.
**Language：** **Colour the (arms/feet/legs/hands). Touch your (ears).**
**Materials：** blue, orange, red and brown colouring pencils or crayons; the outline of a person on paper; coloured beanbags in known colours.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The body song（Track 27）**加新段落：*If you're happy… wave your arms. / stamp your feet.*
**Before the book：** 确保每儿童有红、蓝、黄、棕四支蜡笔；说如 *The blue crayon!* 或 *Blue!* 儿童举起正确颜色；几轮后由一儿童当「老师」。
**With the book（PB p54）*Listen. Colour. Colour the same. Say.*** 指顶部第一个身体部位问 *What are these? Arms? Legs?* 儿童说 Legs；*What colour?* *Listen.* 播音频儿童选正确蜡笔举起，暂停涂色；用顶部其余部位重复。解释儿童需看顶部图的颜色，把下方对应身体部位涂成同色。边涂边巡视。
**Track 35（录音文本）：** *Colour the legs blue.  Colour the hands orange.  Colour the arms red.  Colour the feet brown.*
**After the book：** 画一个人形轮廓，展示蓝、棕、黄、红蜡笔。一儿童说如 *Colour the legs yellow.* 你涂图；用不同儿童针对 ears, eyes, nose, mouth 重复。问完成图：*What colour is the (nose)? What colour are the feet?* 结对接力——结对儿童互看对方完成的画，一个说如 *Arms.* 另一人触他画上的手臂。
**Learning through play — A listen and balance game：** 把已知颜色的豆袋分堆放地上开阔空间（red, blue, yellow, brown）。播音乐儿童走动；暂停说如 *Red on your hand!* 儿童赛跑找正确颜色豆袋放对正确身体部位，看谁放最久；放回豆袋。继续不同指令如 *Yellow on your nose, Blue on your arm, Brown on your ear.*
**Activity Book（p54）：** *Look. Draw. Say.* 指示例图，问 *What colour is his hair? Look at his mouth. Is he happy or sad?* *Say Draw you.* 儿童画自己，边画边告诉你身体部位和颜色，你补全英语词；可问 *Are you happy or sad?* 若儿童愿意，可把身体部位和颜色写（如 red mouth）在图边。可录像展示稍后播放，用 L1 问 *Can you say parts of the body now?*
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Unit 4 开篇视频谈论所学；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 12（Project）—— Pupil's Book page 55

**Objectives：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; Carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** feelings, parts of the body.
**Language：** **Touch your (nose/mouth/ears/eyes). Happy/Sad.**
**Materials：** paper plates; glue; wool or other materials for hair; colouring pencils or crayons; unit objective picture cards; Activity Book stickers; Unit 4 flashcards; puppet; pictures of animals or toy animals (rabbit, elephant, crocodile).
**Skills：** Learning to Learn: Reflecting on and evaluating own learning success: Self-corrects from own stored knowledge when they realise a mistake has been made.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放本单元最爱的歌，Pippa 起舞跟唱。
**Before the book：** 藏一个软玩具兔子或图片，告诉儿童你有动物要他们猜；给线索如 *Small animal. Big feet. Big eyes. Four legs. Big ears.* 并模仿，如 *Good at jumping!* 先猜中（Rabbit!）者拿下玩具/图片；用不同动物重复（elephant: big animal, four legs, small eyes, big feet, big nose, big ears；crocodile: big animal, four legs, no ears, big nose, big mouth）。用类似线索让儿童猜 Pippa 是 mouse（small animal, small nose, small mouth, small eyes, four legs），展示 Pippa 让她说 *It's me! A mouse!* 开课前先用照片和做好的示范纸盘脸（一面笑脸、一面哭脸）展示步骤；给每面展示询问 happy 或 sad，翻面重复。
**With the book（PB p55）*Look. Make. Say.*** 儿童操作：*Draw the eyes. Colour the eyes.* 等指令完成纸盘两面，写上毛做头发；边涂边巡视。随后结对谈论纸盘脸，如 *Red mouth. Mouth. He's happy.*
**After the book：** 发指令如 *Touch your (nose).* 儿童指点纸盘脸的鼻子；请志愿者发指令。*Say Happy! or Sad!* 儿童展示纸盘正确一面。鼓励志愿者为其他身体部位发指令如 *Colour the arms (yellow).*
**Learning to Learn：** 举书快速翻过 Unit 4 说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 4.* 谈论儿童做过的事；鼓励唱会唱的歌、用 L1 复述故事、指身体部位等。自我评估按 TB 40 页流程；儿童把 Unit 4 星星贴纸贴在右上角。
**Activity Book（p55）：** *Point. Say. Colour.* 说句子（如 *They're arms.*）儿童指正确图画，加速并换顺序；发涂色指令，志愿者为其他部位发指令。错误时给儿童时间自我纠正；必要用升调重复错误并停顿让儿童自己纠正。
**Finishing the class：** 逐一指 Unit 4 闪卡让儿童说词；提醒在家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

## Unit 5 Food

单元主题：Food（食物与饮料）。Pupil's Book 页 56–67（原书页 118–141）。

### 单元目标（Unit objectives）

- 熟悉食物与饮料词汇：**apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches**，以及 **juice, milk, water**。
- 学会表达喜好：**I like (apples). I don't like (juice).** 并学说 **Thank you.**。
- 学习字母读音 **a**（the letter sound a，以 apple 为例）。
- 学习数字 **5 和 6**。
- 跨学科（Science）：**identifying fruit（辨认水果）**。
- 故事价值：**Say thank you（有礼貌地道谢）**；阅读故事 *Picky Peter*。
- 复习与进度评估。

---

### Lesson 1（单元开篇歌曲）—— Pupil's Book page 56

**Objective：** Become familiar with the topic of food and listen to a song.
**Vocabulary：** apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches.
**Materials：** real or play food (including apples, bananas, sandwiches, biscuits); flashcards apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches; pre-made unit objective picture cards; puppet; Pippa's house; Unit 5 introduction video; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂。若适龄，在几张桌上布置食物（切片苹果、香蕉、橙子、饼干、面包、胡萝卜——提前向家长/看护人确认有无过敏），也可用玩具食物；让儿童探索、触摸、尝试，走动用英中问 *Do you like this? Which is your favourite? What's this?* 全班投票最喜欢哪种食物。
**Before the book：** 与全班过一遍单元目标（见 TB 19 页，改适 Unit 5）。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，让她见到食物很兴奋；说 *Look, Pippa! Lots of food!* Pippa 说 *Food! Yummy!* 给 Pippa 看几支香蕉问 *What are these?* Pippa 说 *Bananas!* 用 apples, sandwiches, biscuits 重复（无真食物可用玩具食物或闪卡）。Pippa 把所有食物拿进「房子」，说 *Pippa! My food!* 逗全班笑。播放 Unit 5 视频引入主题，暂停提问。
**With the book（PB p56）*Listen to the song.*** 指页中照片说 *Look at the food. Yum!*；指左边照片 *More food! Sandwiches, fruit. Delicious!*；再指一家人做饼干的照片 *Look at the family. They're making biscuits.* 问儿童是否和家人一起做食物、做什么。指页角 Pippa 和 Pop 的图，让 Pippa 说 *Song time! Listen!* 播放 **The food song** 让儿童听，Pippa 可起舞并从「房子」里拿出提到的食物/闪卡。*Listen again.* 播歌儿童指点各段中的相关食物。帮儿童找到 Unit 5 贴纸（sandwiches 和 biscuits）贴到正确位置。
**Track 36 The food song（录音文本）：** *Pippa: Let's sing about food!  Pop: Mmm… Yum!  Pippa: I like sandwiches. I like apples, too. I like food. I like food.  [Pippa and Pop:] Yum, yum, yum, yum, yum, I like food. [x2]  Pop: I like bananas. I like biscuits, too. I like food. I like food.  [Pippa and Pop:] Yum,…*
**After the book：** 再播歌，儿童可摸肚子、舔嘴唇配合 chorus 里的 Yum。指食物说 *Look, food!* 再说 *Look at the food! Pick up different foods…* 谈论（*Yum! I like bananas. Red apples! My favourite. And a big carrot.*）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 2（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 57

**Objective：** Name foods and express likes.
**Vocabulary：** apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches.
**Language：** **I like (apples).**
**Materials：** flashcards apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches; real or play food of the target vocabulary (optional); a plate of cut-up banana (optional); colouring pencils or crayons; puppet.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The food song（Track 36）**儿童起舞跟唱。
**Before the book：** 展示闪卡逐指每个食物数遍，儿童重复；或改用真/玩具食物。叫一志愿者上前，你说一个食物，儿童指正确闪卡/食物；用不同志愿者重复。出示一盘切好的香蕉，吃一片微笑说 *I like bananas.* 或模仿剥皮吃；请一名儿童吃一片或模仿并说 *I like bananas*，用不同儿童重复。
**With the book（PB p57）*Listen. Point. Colour.*** 逐指角色说 *Look! Who is it?* 问一家人在做什么（吃午饭/零食），用动作问 *Who's eating a banana? Who's eating sandwiches? Who's eating apples? What's Dan eating?* 播音频儿童听；再播儿童听指。【*Colour.*】 再播每项后暂停说 Colour，儿童给刚听到的食物涂色（任选颜色）。
**Track 37（录音文本）：** *Kim: Mmm, apples. I like apples.  Mummy: Yes. And bananas. I like bananas.  Daddy: Oh, I like sandwiches. Mmm, sandwiches.  Dan: Biscuits, biscuits, biscuits! Yummy biscuits. I like biscuits!*
**After the book：** 儿童围坐一圈，把食物闪卡发给不同儿童；播音乐传送，音乐停时持卡儿童举起说如 *Bananas!* 或 *I like bananas!*；重复几次。
**Learning through play — A TPR song：** 用「If you're happy and you know it」旋律唱：*If you really like bananas, touch your ears (x2) … Yes, you really like bananas, touch your ears!* 再换 apples（stamp your feet）、sandwiches（wave your arms）、biscuits（touch your nose）；儿童听并做动作，全班一起练再表演。
**Activity Book（p57）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 指着第一个剪影问 *What are these?* 儿童说 Apples，并指出右边已描好的示例图；*Yes. Apples! I like apples.* 儿童只能用指或笔描每行正确食物图（检查握笔和描线方向）；描完后指食物儿童一起说词。
**Learning to Learn：** 用 Pippa 问儿童反思问题（见 TB 第 7 页）做自我评估。
**At home：** 让儿童下次购物时用英语说出食物名。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 3（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 58

**Objective：** Practise expressing likes.
**Vocabulary：** apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches.
**Language：** **I like (apples).**
**Materials：** puppet; real or play food (optional); food flashcards apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches; Pupil's Book stickers; flashcards Kim, Dan (Unit 1) Mummy, Daddy (Unit 2); eight to ten known items (e.g. crayons, a book, pencils, apples, bananas, biscuits, a teddy, balls).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The food song（Track 36）**带动作唱：I like sandwiches（拿想象中的三明治咬一口）、I like apples（拿起苹果咬）、I like bananas（剥香蕉）、I like biscuits（空中画圈）；chorus 摸肚子说 Yum 等。
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa。逐件给 Pippa 看食物（或闪卡），每件 Pippa 说如 *Yum! I like bananas!* Pippa 拿一件食物给你说用 *Thank you, Pippa! I like (apples).*；Pippa 把食物分给不同儿童，他们说 *Apple(s)!* 或 *I like apples!*
**With the book（PB p58）*Listen. Stick. Point. Say.*** 提醒儿童 Kim、Dan 和爸爸妈妈在吃零食。指图问 *Who's this? What are these?* 帮儿童找到书后贴纸页；播音频儿童听指正确贴纸；再播暂停贴好。举起书指 Dan 的图说 *Dan says…* 儿童指饼干贴纸说 *I like biscuits.* 逐个重复。结对练习指点与说。
**Track 38（录音文本）：** *Daddy: I like sandwiches. Yum!  Dan: I like biscuits. Yum!  Mummy: I like bananas. Yum!  Kim: I like apples. Yum! I like bananas!*
**After the book：** 让儿童看书记住每个人喜欢什么，给 30 秒后合书。把角色闪卡贴板上，给一个儿童一张食物闪卡，儿童说如 *Biscuits!* 并放到正确角色旁；再播音频核对。
**Learning through play — A memory game：** 展示八到十件已知物品；逐件举起问 *What's this? / What are these?* 儿童回答。一儿童选一件给 Pippa 说如 *Bananas.* Pippa 说 *Thank you!*；你转开眼让 Pippa 把物品放进「房子」。重复直到全在 Pippa 房里；然后惊讶地说 *Pippa! Where are all the toys? Where's the food? Where are the crayons? They're not yours!* 逗全班笑；儿童喊出 Pippa 房里的东西，Pippa 难过地一件件拿出来说，如 *I like pencils!*
**Activity Book（p58）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童沿底端人像指向他所喜欢食物的连线描线；检查握笔和描线方向。逐指图中人物儿童说如 Biscuits；用 L1 问 *What does she say?* 儿童说 *I like biscuits.*
**Finishing the class：** 播放 **The food song（Track 36）**儿童跟唱做动作；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 4（Sounds and letters）—— Pupil's Book page 59

**Objective：** Practise recognising the letter sound a.
**S&L focus：** Practise letter sound a with the word apple.
**Materials：** pre-made letter card a; flashcard apples; red colouring pencils or crayons; pictures of items or animals which start with the sound a (e.g. ant, alligator, anchor, arrow, astronaut, axe) and others which don't (e.g. teddy, doll, giraffe, shoe, chair); mini whiteboard and marker for each child (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂。说一个食物词并随之按音节拍手，如 Sandwiches（三拍），儿童学拍；用 apples, biscuits, bananas 重复。说儿童知道的词和短语（teddy, sit down, wave your arms, train）混入食物词，儿童跟拍，但听到食物词就停。
**Before the book：** 谈刚才游戏里认真听的重要性。在板上写大号小写 'a' 说字母音 a，全班练几遍；再指字母让儿童自己练音。把预制字母卡 a 在班里传，你喊 *Stop and say!* 持卡儿童发字母音。以同样方法教词里的音：把 apples 闪卡放字母 'a' 旁，指并说 apples、发 a 音，再指读数遍让儿童跟读。
**With the book（PB p59）*Listen. Trace. Colour. Say.*** *Listen and point!* 播音频儿童指字母或苹果图。*Trace.* 显示字母 a 的轮廓，让儿童用指或红蜡笔/铅笔描；把苹果涂红，与描字母所用颜色一致。边走动边听儿童指字母和苹果、发音并说词。
**Track 39（录音文本）：** *a — apple apple apple — a — apple* 等。
**After the book：** *Look and listen: 'a' like 'apple' or no?* 展示一系列图，有的以 a 开头、有的不是；逐个说词并夸大首音，儿童对以 a 开头的喊 Apple!。表扬认真听。若进度合适，用空中手指书空字母 a 的形状，再用板上的字母示范如何描；儿童可用迷你白板笔练习写 a。
**Activity Book（p59）：** *Listen again. Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 再播音频儿童描字母 'a' 并圈出以 a 开头的图（苹果）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 5（Literacy：故事）—— Pupil's Book pages 60–61

**Objective：** Listen to, enjoy and answer questions about a story.
**Story language：** apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches, picky, **I like (apples). I don't like (apples). Thank you, Mummy.**
**Materials：** whistle (optional); Big Book; real or play food you dislike (or pictures); Unit 5 literacy story video.
**Starting the class：** 儿童在教室移动，你吹哨就停。*Say I like bananas!* 儿童模仿吃香蕉；用 apples/sandwiches/biscuits 重复。用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 打开 Big Book 让儿童找标题，用 L1 解释 picky 的意思（不喜欢很多不同食物的人）；展示你不喜欢的食物说如 *I'm picky. I don't like pasta. I don't like sushi. I don't like pineapple.* 指男孩说 *Look! A boy. It's Peter. Picky Peter.* 指女人问 *Who's this?*（Mummy.）用 L1 问第一幅图 Peter 为何不开心（他不喜欢那些食物）。儿童说出图中会用英语说的食物词。
**With the book（PB p60–61）*Listen.*** 儿童先看每幅图说出他们认为发生什么，你改述成英语（*Mummy's making a sandwich. Yes! There's banana in the sandwich. Peter likes it!*）。*Now let's listen to the story.* 播音频儿童看 Big Book，逐帧指图。每幅后提问：*Is Peter happy?*（No.）*What does he say in this picture?*（'I don't like…'）*What does Mummy do?*（She makes a sandwich.）*Does Peter like the sandwich?*（Yes, he does.）*Does Peter like apples and bananas now?*（Yes.）*Why?*（Because Mummy put them with other foods and he tried them.）问儿童喜欢三明治里的哪些食物，试过香蕉三明治或苹果饼干吗。
**Track 40 —— Picky Peter（录音文本）：** *Peter is Picky.  Peter: No, thank you. I don't like apples and I don't like bananas.  Mummy makes a sandwich.  Mummy makes biscuits.  Peter: Yum! I like sandwiches.  Thank you, Mummy.  Peter: What's this, Mummy?  Mummy: It's banana.  Mummy makes biscuits.  Peter: Yum! I like biscuits.  Thank you, Mummy.  Peter: What's this, Mummy?  Mummy: It's apple.  Peter: Wow! I like apples and I like bananas!  Thank you, Mummy.*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍配拟声动作，检查儿童记得 Thank you 的意思；或播放读写故事视频。*IN this scene at the top*：指图说 *Look! Peter and his Mummy. Peter likes…?* 儿童说 Apples, biscuits, bananas, sandwiches.
**Activity Book（p60）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 逐指小图，你说故事中的句子如 *I like bananas.* 儿童指食物图；儿童描下面 Peter 喜欢的食物（全部物品）。
**Finishing the class：** *What was the problem? What did Mummy do? What happened in the end?* 儿童用 L1 复述故事。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 60–61

**Objective：** Recognise the value of saying thank you, from the story.
**Value：** **Say thank you（有礼貌地道谢）。**
**Skills：** Communication: Using appropriate language and register for context: Uses simple, polite forms of greetings, introductions and farewells (i.e. saying hello, please, thank you and sorry).
**Story language：** apples, bananas, biscuits, sandwiches, picky, **I like (apples). I don't like (apples). Thank you, Mummy.**
**Materials：** soft toy or play food; Big Book; colouring pencils or crayons; play food; tea sets or plastic jugs, cups, plates, etc.; phone/tablet (optional).
**Starting the class：** 儿童围坐一圈，给一个儿童软玩具或玩具食物，鼓励他们说 *Thank you.*；该儿童传给左边的人，接住的孩子说 *Thank you* 再传。玩具传开后对另一儿童给不同玩具，说 *Thank you* 并传，依次加更多玩具进圈。
**Before the book：** 一起看 Unit 5 读写故事视频，暂停问 *Who is picky? Is he picky at the end of the story?*
**With the book *Listen.*** 请不同儿童给你递物品，如 *Yolanda, a book, please.* 收到时说 *Thank you!*；请儿童指出图中 Peter 礼貌（说 Thank you）的画面。问如果不喜欢某种食物又想礼貌怎么说（*No, thank you.*），指出 Peter 对妈妈一直很有礼貌；可讨论在不太熟悉的人面前拒绝食物是否礼貌。
**After the book：** 先用英语再说 L1 *It's important to say 'thank you'.* 谈礼貌的重要性，请儿童举例日常生活中何时说 'thank you'。*IN the book*：用英中谈图，儿童说谁在道谢，如 *Look! The man's got a sandwich. The boy says…（thank you）The girl says…?* 用动作协助。问 Peter 是否勇敢尝试妈妈做的新食物，谈尝试新东西常是好主意。
**Activity Book（p61）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 儿童找出并涂上图中正在道谢的人。
**Learning through play — Saying thank you：** 给每组儿童布置茶具或玩具食物（杯、盘等）；儿童一起开茶话会，鼓励分享、互相有礼（一个倒茶、另一个说 Thank you!）；可用英语点自己认识的食物如 *Banana, please!*；边玩边鼓励尽量礼貌。可录像稍后播放。
**At home：** 让儿童在家有人对他们好或给他们食物饮料时说 thank you。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 62

**Objective：** Name drinks and express likes and dislikes.
**Vocabulary：** juice, milk, water.
**Language：** **I (don't like) (juice).**
**Materials：** Unit 5 flashcards; real drinks (juice, milk, water) (optional, if appropriate); Unit 5 character story video; puppet.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；边念 *Pat-a-cake, Pat-a-cake, Baker's man* 边拍出模式让儿童模仿（前面拍手、两侧各一拍配 Pat-a-cake；双手拍大腿配 Baker's man），重复直到儿童学会。画字母 'a' 在板上，念字母音 a 时指点。
**Before the book：** 展闪卡逐指每个饮品数遍，儿童重复（或用真饮品）；玩发音游戏（见 TB 13 页）；叫一志愿者上前说一个饮品，儿童指正确饮品，用不同志愿者重复。喝口奶微笑说 *I like milk*；喝口果汁皱眉说 *I don't like juice.* 或配模仿与闪卡；重复几次。给不同儿童饮品（或闪卡）问如 *Juice?* 儿童可答 *Yes, please* 或 *No, thank you*（提前检查食物过敏）。
**With the book（PB p62）*Listen. Point. Trace.*** 提醒儿童 Pop 很热很渴，Pippa 给他不同饮品；引导说出每幅图中的饮品。播音频儿童听；*Listen and point.* 再播儿童听指饮品和胶管中的水。*Say Trace.* 指轮廓解释儿童需描每个饮品图。
**Track 41（录音文本，含梦境引子）：** *[Dream sequence introduction]  Pop: Phew!  Pippa: Hello Pop. Juice?  Pop: No, thank you, Pippa. I don't like juice.  Pippa: Milk?  Pop: No, thank you. I don't like milk!  Pop: AAAAGGGHHHH!  Pippa: Ha! Ha!  Pop: I like water!*
**After the book:** 播放角色故事视频，每个场景暂停指不同饮品问 *What's this?* 用 L1 问 *What's happening?* 叫成对儿童上板：Child A 给 Child B 逐一展示闪卡，Child B 说 *I like…* 或 *I don't like…*，Child A 把卡放入正确栏。*Finishing the class*：在板中央画竖线，左边画笑脸、右边画哭脸；演示：把一项食物/饮品闪卡给 Pippa，Pippa 说 *I don't like milk.* 并放到哭脸侧；继续更多卡。Pippa 指闪卡并给全班鼓掌（或你指并拍）。
**Activity Book（p62）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童描线完成饮品图；边描边问 *What's this?* 描完后指饮品让全班说词，再以不同顺序指（water, milk, juice）儿童记忆按序说词。
**Finishing the class：** 展示饮品闪卡指图读词鼓励跟上；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice with a chant）—— Pupil's Book page 63

**Objective：** Practise talking about food and drink with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** apples, bananas, biscuits, juice, milk, sandwiches, water.
**Language：** **I (don't like) (juice).**
**Materials：** string or tape; Unit 5 flashcards; real food and drink (optional); yellow, red, blue and brown colouring pencils or crayons; Unit 5 chant video.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；用绳子或胶带在教室/操场中央画一条线。展示一个食物或饮品闪卡（如 biscuits）说词，做出思考、微笑的样子走到线一侧说 *I like biscuits.*；用另一张卡换到另一侧说 *I don't like…*；展示闪卡儿童按自己喜好跳到或跑到线正确一侧；两侧各选一儿童造句（如 *I like bananas. / I don't like bananas.*）。
**Before the book：** 展示食物与饮品闪卡（或真物）问不同儿童如 *Milk?* 他们答 *I like milk* 或 *I don't like milk.*
**With the book（PB p63）*Listen. Colour. Sing.*** 指女孩说 *Look! A girl!* 逐指照片问 *What's this? / What are these?* 儿童说如 Juice!。用 L1 解释儿童需听出女孩喜欢/不喜欢哪些食物饮品。播 chant 只让儿童听；再播，需时暂停让儿童给每个食物/饮品涂正确表情（查问 *Juice. Like or don't like?*）她喜欢 juice, sandwiches, biscuits, water, apples；不喜欢 bananas 和 milk。播重点儿童指食物饮品；再播让他们跟上并/或配合吃喝动作，I like 段落微笑、I don't like 段落皱眉。
**Track 42 The food chant：** *I like juice, yum, yum, yum, I like sandwiches. I like biscuits and water, too. I like apples, yum, yum, yum!  I don't like bananas. I don't like milk!  Oh no! Oh no! Oh no!  [I don't like bananas. I don't like milk!]  [Repeat]*
**After the book：** 再播 The food chant 教每样食物饮品动作；儿童跟动作、尽量跟词。*IN pairs*：儿童轮流造句说图中人物说的话。用自我评估策略（TB 第 7 页）让儿童表示对表现的感觉。
**Activity Book（p63）：** *Look. Colour. Say.* 指页顶色键问如 *What colour is the banana?*（Yellow!）用其他食物饮品重复；儿童用色键涂图。全班发指令，如 *Colour the bananas…（yellow!）* 边涂边问个别 *What colour is the juice?*
**Finishing the class：** 播 The food chant 视频鼓励跟上；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers 数字）—— Pupil's Book page 64

**Objective：** Practise counting using numbers 1–6.
**Vocabulary：** numbers 5, 6.
**Language：** **(Five) (apples).**
**Materials：** whistle (optional); four pictures of individual children per pair (optional); puppet; blue and yellow colouring pencils or crayons; a bowl with six apples.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；高声从 1 数到 4 几遍，举起手指表示数字，儿童模仿。移到开阔空间（如可能），儿童跑动，你吹哨就停；*Say Listen!* 发指令分组如 *Three in a group!* 儿童尽快组队；用不同数字重复；或结对举起正确数量的儿童图片。
**Before the book：** 在板上写数字 1–4。儿童唤出 Pippa，Pippa 指数字数 *One, two, three, four！* 儿童跟着数。Pippa 指一个数字并点一名儿童，该儿童说如 One，Pippa 指不同数字点名每人说一个。举起不同数量的物品（1–4，如四支铅笔）和儿童一起大声数 *One, two, three, four. Four pencils.* 加一件物品数到 5，在板上写 5；以同样方式呈现数字 6。
**With the book（PB p64）*Listen. Count. Colour.*** 与全班指图一起大声数三明治和香蕉。儿童描数字（用指或铅笔，提醒从顶部开始），再描与上方数字匹配的那组食物。完成后儿童举书一起说：*Five. Five apples. Six. Six bananas.*
**Track 43（录音文本）：** *One, two, three, four, five. Five apples.（拍手五下）One, two, three, four, five, six. Six biscuits.（拍手六下）*
**After the book:** 用 L1 说 *Listen! How many?* 拍 1 到 6 次手，儿童说如 *Four!*；变换拍手次数重复。
**Learning through play — A counting rhyme：** 把六个苹果放碗里和儿童大声数，说 *Six apples!*；唱下面的童谣边演边拿苹果：*Six apples in the bowl, One fell out – roll, roll, roll, How many apples in the bowl? 1, 2, 3, 4, 5!* 再从头开始（Five apples in the bowl…）；问 *How many apples in the bowl?*（Zero!）放回苹果再唱一遍。
**Activity Book（p64）：** *Look. Count. Trace. Say.* 儿童找出并描计数；播放 **The food chant（Track 42）**让儿童跟上。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 10（Science：Identifying fruit）—— Pupil's Book page 65

**Objective：** Identify fruit.
**Vocabulary：** fruit, apple, banana, biscuits, carrot, eggs, orange, pineapple, melon, eggs, chicken; sandwiches, strawberries.
**Language：** **Fruit? Yes. / No.**
**Materials：** real food, play food or pictures of food, including fruit and vegetables (e.g. bananas, apples, sandwiches, biscuits, strawberries, oranges, carrots, potatoes, pineapple, melon, eggs, chicken); colouring pencils or crayons.
**Skills：** Critical Thinking: Understanding and analysing links between ideas: Sorts, arranges and describes objects by shape, size, colour, weight, texture and position.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The food chant（Track 42）**儿童跟上做动作。
**Before the book：** 展示一堆真/玩具食物或照片（含水果蔬菜），说如 *Yum! I like apples! I don't like potatoes.* 举起一个苹果问 *Fruit?* *Say Yes, fruit.* 用不同水果（bananas, pineapple, melon）重复；可教新食物词，但儿童只需认哪些是 fruit 并答 Yes。举起一些饼干问 *Fruit?* 说 No，用不是水果的食物重复。展示不同食物问 *Fruit?* 全班答 Yes/No；确保儿童明白蔬菜不是水果（用 L1）。用 L1 问儿童还知道哪些水果并喜欢（改述成英语）。
**With the book（PB p65）*Look. Colour.*** 说 *Look! Colour the fruit.* 儿童给显示水果的照片框涂色，告诉儿童用同色蜡笔，清楚说明这些都是水果同一类。
**After the book：** 儿童围坐一圈，分发玩具食物（或图片），确保水果数量为偶数。播音乐传食物，音乐停时喊 Fruit! *Change places!* 持水果的儿童起立与另一个站起的儿童换座；重复几次，换完座后可让持水果者说如 *Bananas!* 或 *I like bananas. 播放 Unit 5 视频再次观察并让儿童每次看到水果就拍手。
**Activity Book（p65）：** *Look. Find. Colour. Say.* 儿童只看图并只涂水果；涂后指说 Apple – fruit. Banana – fruit. 帮他们说出不熟悉的水果：Strawberry – fruit. Orange – fruit. 核对：逐指照片问 Fruit? 儿童举书显示是否涂了框。
**At home：** 让儿童在厨房找水果，和家里人说，下次课可带最爱水果做 show-and-tell。
**Finishing the class：** 把开课时的食物放入袋子或盒子，问儿童记得哪些食物（可用英语或 L1），逐件拿出。请志愿者上前拿一个喜欢的食物展示并说如 *I like sandwiches.* 用下课常规结束，一起收拾。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 66

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** food and drink.
**Language：** **I (like/don't like) (juice/milk/water/sandwiches/biscuits).**
**Materials：** Unit 5 flashcards; colouring pencils or crayons; phone/tablet (optional); pictures of food and drink (e.g. biscuits, juice, water, an apple, a banana, sandwiches—the same items for each team); a box for each team.
**Skills：** Social Responsibilities: Understanding and describing own and others' cultures: Identifies similarities and differences between self and others (e.g. favourite pastimes, toys, colours, food etc.).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The food chant（Track 42）**加入儿童建议的食物饮品新段落。
**Before the book：** 用闪卡游戏（见 TB 13 页）复习食物饮品词。
**With the book（PB p66）*Look. Find. Colour. Say.*** 指色键里的食物饮品，儿童说词并用 I like…/I don't like… 造句；解释儿童需在大图中找出并涂色。
**After the book：** 问大图里的物品，如 *Where are the biscuits? Where's the milk?* 儿童指出。志愿者上前指图物品、说词并说自己是否喜欢。儿童结对练指点说词或作更多 I like/don't like 句子。
**Learning through play — A run and find game：** 组队每组坐在一名坐椅子的儿童后面排队；每队前放一个盒子，装同样的食物饮品物品（够远可供跑）。说一个食物饮品词，如 *3, 2, 1… Sandwiches!* 队首儿童跑去盒子找出该物品交给你，回到队尾坐下；第一个交对并坐下的队得分。每人都坐好后举起物品问 *What's this? / What are these?* 下一名儿童坐到队前。可在盒里放干扰物（教室物品或玩具）。
**Activity Book（p66）：** *Look. Draw. Say.* 指示例图（右上），用 L1 解释儿童画了他们喜欢的食物；问 *What are these?*（Bananas.）儿童画自己和自己喜欢的食物，边画边告诉你英文的食物词（或你补充新词）；完成后展示图说如 *I like apples.* 若别的儿童也画了苹果，起立说 *I like apples!* 可录像稍后播放。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Unit 5 视频谈论所学；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 12（Project）—— Pupil's Book page 67

**Objectives：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; Carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** food and drink.
**Language：** **I like (apples/bananas).**
**Materials：** Unit 5 flashcards; real or play food and drink (optional); puppet; photocopiable plant/tree templates, pages 250–251; pre-made unit objective picture cards; colouring pencils or crayons; glue; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在教室各处摆放多件真/玩具食物饮品（或闪卡）。儿童走动，吹哨停。说如 *Find some bananas!* 儿童赛跑拿起正确物品举高说如 *I like bananas!* 若是可数就大声与全班数总数（*One, two, three, four, five bananas!*）；用不同食物饮品重复。
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa。叫一志愿者选一种食物饮品给 Pippa，说如 *Biscuits?* Pippa 说 *I like biscuits! / I don't like biscuits.*，对喜欢的食物显得十分兴奋、对不喜欢的十分厌恶逗全班；Pippa 然后问 *You?* 鼓励儿童造句如 *I like biscuits.*；用不同志愿者重复。开课前复印/打印并裁剪苹果树和香蕉树模板（每个儿童至少一份），用模板各做一个成品苹果树和香蕉树；向儿童展示模型让他们辨认不同水果。
**With the book（PB p67）*Look. Make. Say.*** 用照片展示做香蕉树/苹果树的步骤；每个儿童选做香蕉树或苹果树（按更喜欢的果子）；边涂边巡视，然后协助粘贴，与个别儿童数出香蕉/苹果并问 *What colour is this?* 完成后儿童展示树/果树并说 *I like bananas* 或 *I like apples.*
**After the book：** 可把完成的树/果树做成教室展示（绿地上、蓝天下）；给儿童时间看展示并谈所见。
**Learning to Learn：** 举书快速翻过 Unit 5 说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 5.* 用目标图片卡谈论儿童做过的事、问喜欢/不喜欢哪些活动；鼓励唱歌段、用 L1 复述故事、说自己喜欢的食物饮品。自我评估按 TB 40 页流程；儿童把 Unit 5 星星贴纸贴在右上角。*Activity Book（p67）：* *Point. Say. Colour.* 结对儿童对每样物品说一句对自己成立的句子，如 *I like milk. I don't like water.* 边练边巡视；错误时给时间自我纠正，必要时用升调重复错误并停顿让儿童自纠。
**Finishing the class：** 逐一指 Unit 5 闪卡让儿童说词；提醒在家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

## Unit 6 Animals

单元主题：Animals（动物）。Pupil's Book 页 68–79（原书页 142–165）。

### 单元目标（Unit objectives）

- 熟悉动物词汇：**cat, dog, fish, rabbit**。
- 学会问与答位置：**Where's the (cat)? Here it is. It's (under) the (chair).**
- 学习字母读音 **e**（the letter sound e，以 elephant 为例）。
- 学习数字 **7 和 8**。
- 跨学科（Science）：**What animals need（动物需要什么）**。
- 故事价值：**Be kind to animals（善待动物）**；阅读故事 *Emma's new cat*。
- 复习与进度评估。

---

### Lesson 1（单元开篇歌曲）—— Pupil's Book page 68

**Objective：** Become familiar with the topic of animals and listen to a song.
**Vocabulary：** cat, dog, fish, rabbit.
**Materials：** toy animals; pre-made unit objective picture cards; puppet; flashcards rabbit, dog, fish, cat; pictures of different types of rabbit, dog, fish, cat; Unit 6 introduction video; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在教室不同区域摆放玩具动物（软玩具、埋在沙区的塑料动物或「幸运袋」桶中），儿童自由选择玩；边玩边用英语交谈，如 *What colour is this? Let's count the cows. (One, two, three, four, five!) Is the rabbit big or small?*
**Before the book：** 与全班过一遍单元目标（见 TB 19 页，改适 Unit 6）。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，让她见到玩具动物很兴奋说 *Animals!* 给 Pippa 看 rabbit 闪卡问 *What's this?* Pippa 说 *A rabbit!* 用 dog 和 fish 重复；再给 Pippa 看 cat 闪卡，Pippa 边喊 *A cat! A cat! A cat!* 边跑开躲进「房子」。展示更多动物图（不同种类/颜色）重复说词，鼓励儿童说他们养或想养哪种宠物。播放 Unit 6 视频引入主题，暂停问儿童看到什么、以为本单元学什么。
**With the book（PB p68）*Listen to the song.*** 指照片说 *Look! Pets! A dog! It's got a ball. It's running… The boy's got a pet. It's a fish… Look! More pets! A cat and a rabbit.* 问儿童会选哪种宠物及原因。指页角 Pippa 和 Pop 的图，让 Pippa 说 *Song time! Listen!* 播放 **The animals song** 让儿童听，指所提到的动物闪卡；再播儿童指点各段相关动物。帮儿童找到 Unit 6 贴纸（cat 和 rabbit）贴到正确位置。
**Track 44 The animals song（录音文本）：** *Pop: Let's sing…  Pippa: …about animals!  Pop: OK!  Adult: Where's the cat? Where's the cat? Please find the cat.  Child: Here it is. Here it is. Here it is.  Adult: Where's the dog?…  Adult: Where's the rabbit?…  Adult: Where's the fish?…*
**After the book：** 再播歌鼓励儿童为不同动物做动作（cat 用「爪子」洗脸、dog 跑动、rabbit 跳跃、fish 张嘴闭嘴）；再播跟唱起舞。*Finishing the class*：收走所有玩具和闪卡并说词，然后唤 Pippa 说 *Pippa, it's OK! No cats! There are no cats!* 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 2（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 69

**Objective：** Name animals and ask where something is.
**Vocabulary：** cat, dog, fish, rabbit.
**Language：** **Where's the (cat)? Here it is.**
**Materials：** flashcards cat, dog, fish, rabbit, Tinks; soft or plastic toy cat, dog, fish, rabbit (optional); puppet; red, blue, brown and yellow colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The animals song（Track 44）**儿童做动作跟唱。
**Before the book：** 展示宠物闪卡逐一指每个动物数遍，儿童重复（或用软玩具教）。以不同顺序问动物：*Where's the fish? Where's the dog? Where's the cat? Where's the rabbit?* 儿童指正确动物。叫一志愿者上前说一个动物，儿童指正确闪卡或拿起玩具；用不同志愿者重复。把闪卡/玩具发给不同儿童，每个儿童说词再传给下一个人，依次下去。藏起一张闪卡/玩具，叫出 Pippa 四处寻找并问 *Where's the (cat)?* 逗笑全班；Pippa 找到动物说 *Here it is. Hello, (cat)!*，用其他动物重复。
**With the book（PB p69）*Listen. Point. Circle.*** 指角色问 *Who is it?* 问儿童在哪、在做什么（在花园找动物）。问花园里有什么动物：*Where's the rabbit? Where's the dog? And the fish? Where's the cat?* 播音频儿童听；再播儿童听指动物（检查找对）；再播暂停让儿童圈出动物。问 cat 的名字（Tinks），展示 Tinks 闪卡练习名字。
**Track 45（录音文本）：** *Kim: Hmm. Where's the rabbit?  Dan: Here it is! Hello, rabbit!  Kim: Where's the dog?  Dan: Umm… Ah! Here it is. Hello, dog!  Kim: Where's the fish?  Dan: Oh! Here it is. Hello, fish!  Kim: Where's the cat?  Dan: Hmm. Cat?  Kim: Yes, the cat!  Dan: Aw… Here it is. Hello, Tinks!*
**After the book：** 请一志愿者上前闭眼，藏起一张动物闪卡/玩具，说如 *Open your eyes. Where's the rabbit?* 儿童环视找到，向全班展示说 *Here it is. The rabbit.* 请志愿者数动物并说（One. One fish. / One, two. Two cats. …）
**Activity Book（p69）：** *Look. Colour. Count. Say.* 指色键问如 *What colour is the rabbit?*（Red.）儿童举起正确蜡笔；儿童用色键给主图动物涂色；边涂边问 *What colour is it?* 然后全班一起数动物。选志愿者指数说（One. One fish. / One, two. Two cats. / One, two. Two rabbits. One, two, three. Three dogs.）
**Learning to Learn：** 用 Pippa 用 L1 问反思问题，如 *What new words do you know? What was your favourite word? What was the best activity today? What was hard?*（更多见 TB 第 7 页）。
**At home：** 让儿童与家人谈自己喜欢的动物。
**Finishing the class：** 展示闪卡，让 Pippa 指图带读鼓励跟上；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 3（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 70

**Objective：** Practise talking about where things are.
**Vocabulary：** cat, dog, fish, rabbit.
**Language：** **Where's the (cat)? Here it is.**
**Materials：** flashcards cat, dog, fish, rabbit; ball, doll, teddy, train (Unit 3); book, crayon, pencil (Unit 1) or real toys, soft toy animals and classroom objects/pictures (enough for one per child); Pupil's Book stickers; six items (e.g. toy train, teddy, doll, ball, book, banana).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The animals song（Track 44）**带动作唱，鼓励跟唱。
**Before the book：** 展示动物闪卡或软玩具，叫一志愿者上前问如 *Where's the cat?* 儿童指正确闪卡或拿起软玩具说 *Here it is.*；用不同志愿者重复。
**With the book（PB p70）*Listen. Stick. Point. Say.*** 问图里的动物：*Where's the (fish)?* 儿童指正确图，举书核对说 *Yes! Here it is!* 帮儿童找到书后贴纸页；播音频儿童听指正确贴纸；再播暂停贴好。描完后逐指左边每个动物一起问 *Where's the (rabbit)?* 手指沿着线到右边匹配图一起说 *Here it is.* 结对练问与答。
**Track 46（录音文本）：** *Kim: Where's the cat?  Dan: Here it is.  Kim: Where's the rabbit?  Dan: Here it is!  Kim: Where's the dog?  Dan: Here it is.  Kim: Where's the fish?  Dan: Here it is.*
**After the book：** 展示玩具、宠物和教室物品的混合闪卡（或用实物和图片），确保班里每人一件。先展示六件已知物品问 *What's this?* 然后逐一点名儿童上前问不同问题，如 *Where's the small teddy?* 儿童指或拿起物品说 *Here it is.* 并带回座位。当每人都有物品后，问如 *Where's the blue ball?* 持正确物品的儿童拿回说 Here it is。结对练问与答（如 *Where's the dog? Here it is.*），巡视记录需进一步练习者。*Learning through play — A hide and seek game*：两名 seeker 闭眼，其余儿童安静帮你把物品藏在教室各处；seeker 睁眼寻找，全班问如 *Where's the banana?* 找到时儿童说 *Here it is!* 换物品和不同 seeker 重复。
**Activity Book（p70）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童沿连线匹配动物（检查握笔和从左到右描线）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 4（Sounds and letters）—— Pupil's Book page 71

**Objective：** Practise recognising the letter sound e.
**S&L focus：** Practise letter sound e with the word elephant.
**Materials：** flashcards cat, dog, fish, rabbit; frog, bird (Unit 9); Pop, Pippa (Unit 1); pre-made letter card e; pictures of items or animals which start with the sound e (e.g. elephant, egg, elf, elbow) and others which don't (e.g. ant, apple, doll, giraffe, shoe, chair); mini whiteboard and marker for each child (optional); recording of the song Nelly the elephant (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；放音乐让儿童跳舞，音乐停出示一张宠物闪卡，儿童说词并像那种动物一样移动（含 Pop、Pippa、bird、frog 的卡）；音乐再起正常跳舞，重复不同闪卡。
**Before the book：** 在板上写大号小写 'e' 说字母音 e，儿童重复练几遍；再指字母让儿童自己练。把预制字母卡 e 在班里传，*Stop and say!* 持卡儿童发音。把大象图放字母 'e' 旁，指说 elephant 发 e 音；再指让儿童说词和音（或传一只软玩具大象，儿童说 elephant, e），重复几遍。
**With the book（PB p71）*Listen. Trace. Colour. Say.*** *Listen and point!* 播音频儿童指字母或大象图，核对指对。*Trace.* 用空中手指书空 e 形状，再用板上的字母示范如何描（从中线左到右、再逆时针）；儿童用指或红蜡笔描页面上的字母 e。说 red 并指出 e 音出现在词中间。把大象涂色，用红蜡笔涂耳朵内侧。边走动边听儿童指字母和大象、发音或说词。
**Track 47（录音文本）：** *e — elephant elephant elephant — e — elephant* 等。
**After the book：** *Look and listen: 'e' like 'elephant' or no?* 告诉儿童听到以 e 开头的词就模仿大象（跺脚、一支手臂作象鼻）；展示一系列图，有的以 e 开头、有的不是，逐个说词夸大首音，儿童只为 e 开头的模仿大象。表扬认真听。若进度合适，儿童用迷你白板笔练习写 e。
**Activity Book（p71）：** *Listen again. Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 再播音频儿童描字母 e 并圈出以 e 开头的图（大象）。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 **The animals song（Track 44）**儿童跟上做动作；或播放 Nelly the elephant 录音，儿童随音乐像大象移动。*Note：* 请儿童下次课带自己宠物的照片（提前告知家长/看护人）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 5（Literacy：故事）—— Pupil's Book pages 72–73

**Objective：** Listen to, enjoy and answer questions about a story.
**Story language：** food, new, **Kitty is under the (chair/table). I'm sorry.**
**Materials：** large 'cat' paw prints drawn on card (optional); photos of your pets; Big Book; flashcard cat; Unit 6 literacy story video.
**Starting the class：** 在地面摆大猫爪印；放音乐，儿童像猫一样从爪印走到爪印并学猫叫。用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 展示你和儿童宠物的照片，逐个问宠物情况（名字、年龄、爱做什么）并谈论照顾宠物。打开 Big Book 让儿童找标题，解释 new 的意思。展示 cat 闪卡让儿童说词，解释 kitty 是小猫的名字。指图 1 的女孩说 *Look! This girl is Emma.* 指她父母问 *Who's this?*（Mummy and Daddy.）问一家人在哪（在动物收容所）；可解释无家可归的猫由收容所照顾，家庭可以收养一只。儿童说出每幅图中发生什么，问 Emma 和小猫的感受。
**With the book（PB p72–73）*Listen.*** 播音频儿童看 Big Book，逐帧指图。每幅后提问：*Does Emma like Kitty?*（Yes.）*Where's Kitty?*（Under the chair.）*Why?* *What's Emma got for Kitty?*（Food.）*Is Kitty OK now?*（Yes.）*Why?*（Because Emma is being more gentle.）问 Emma 如何照顾 Kitty（给她家和食物水、学着温柔）。
**Track 48 —— Emma's new cat（录音文本）：** *Emma has got a new cat.  Emma: Oh! I like you, Kitty!  Emma: Let's play, Kitty!  Uh-oh! Where's the cat? Here it is. It's under the chair.  Emma has got food for Kitty.  Emma: Here, Kitty!  Emma has got water for Kitty.  Emma: Here, Kitty!  Emma has got a toy for Kitty.  Emma: Here, Kitty!  Look! Where's Kitty now? Here! Kitty likes Emma!  Emma: Oh! Yay! Hello, Kitty, hello!  Uh-oh. Kitty's under the table.  Emma: I'm sorry, Kitty!*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍，用录音语言、拟声与动作；教 chair 和 table 并解释 I'm sorry 的意思；或播放读写故事视频。*IN the bottom scene*：指场景 *Look! Kitty's under the chair.* What's Emma got for Kitty?（a toy.）问 Emma 还给 Kitty 什么（food, water）；儿童描圈出食物、水和玩具。
**Activity Book（p72）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 指底部图，说故事中的句子（Kitty is under the chair. Emma's got food/water/a toy for Kitty.），儿童指点正确图。
**Finishing the class：** 用 L1 问 *Why did Kitty hide? What did Emma have for Kitty? What happened in the end?* 鼓励儿童用 L1 复述故事。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 72–73

**Objective：** Recognise the value of being kind to animals, from the story.
**Value：** **Be kind to animals（善待动物）。**
**Skills：** Social Responsibilities: Understanding personal responsibilities as part of a group and in society—including citizenship: Learns to care for animals and plants.
**Story language：** food, new, **Kitty is under the (chair/table). I'm sorry.**
**Materials：** soft toy animals (including a cat); Big Book; bag and soft toy animal; colouring pencils or crayons; props for a vet's role-play (e.g. boxes, cages, play stethoscopes, towels, rubber gloves, white shirts, small torches, notepads and pencils, animal posters); phone/tablet (optional); real items used for pets (e.g. leads, collars, water bottles, brushes, food or water dishes) or pictures.
**Starting the class：** 出示软玩具猫说 *Look, it's Kitty! Move like Kitty.* 模仿猫走路，儿童模仿；*Wash your ears like Kitty!*、*Stretch your legs like Kitty. Sleep like Kitty. Play like Kitty. Climb like Kitty. Eat your food like Kitty. Drink some water like Kitty.* 以不同顺序重复，儿童听做。用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 一起看 Unit 6 读写故事视频，用 L1 问 *What happens at the beginning of the story?*
**With the book *Listen.*** 用教室物品强化 under 的意思。展示 Big Book 让儿童指出 Emma 善待新猫的画面。用 L1 问 Emma 怎么靠近 Kitty 才不会吓到它（安静温柔）；谈见到新宠物时不要在主人说可以前先摸它，然后要善良好。
**After the book：** 把软玩具动物放进袋子，告诉儿童动物很紧张所以躲着；问儿童该怎么做，轮流善待它（安静走向它、给它水/食物/玩具、轻轻唤它）；儿童善待时把玩具慢慢拿出袋子，然后在班里传给大家抚摸，鼓励像对真动物一样的温柔安静。展示照顾宠物用的真物品或图片，问儿童动物是什么、物品是什么（如 dog–lead）；传物品问谁家里有。
**Activity Book（p73）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 问图中能看到哪些宠物（rabbits）；用英语谈图（*Look! This boy's got water for the rabbit. He likes the rabbit. The rabbit likes the boy. It's happy. Oh dear! This rabbit is under the table. The girl says 'I'm sorry, rabbit!'*）；儿童涂上图中善待动物的人（除吵嚷的女孩外所有人都涂）。
**Learning through play — A vet's role-play：** 搭建一个兽医角色扮演区（盒子、笼子、玩具听诊器、毛巾、橡皮手套、白衬衫、小手电、便签笔、动物海报）；巡视参与，鼓励儿童温柔对待动物；可录像稍后播放。
**At home：** 让儿童告诉家人如何善待动物。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 74

**Objective：** Name furniture and talk about where things are.
**Vocabulary：** chair, table, on, under.
**Language：** **It's (under) the (chair).**
**Materials：** Unit 6 flashcards; Unit 6 character story video; puppet; a known soft toy animal for each pair of children.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The animals song（Track 44）**儿童做动作，用宠物闪卡作提示并模仿 Where's the…? 的提问。
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa。Pippa 坐在你椅子里放松说 *Ah! My chair!* 说 *Pippa! That's my chair!* 逗笑；Pippa 坐到桌子上同样反应。全班练 table 和 chair。Pippa 指桌、椅问 *What's this?* 儿童说 Table! 或 Chair!。让 Pippa 坐椅子上说 *Pippa's on the chair. On. On the chair. Pippa's on the chair.* 儿童重复；把 Pippa 放桌上同样练；再把 Pippa 放椅子下练 *Under. Under the chair.*，桌下练 under 于 table。Pippa 把不同教室物品放桌上/桌下，每次造一句让儿童重复（It's on the chair. / It's under the table. 等）；请志愿者把物品放桌上/桌下造句。给 Pippa 看 Tinks 闪卡，她跑开；问儿童猫的名字，问 *Does Pippa like Tinks?*
**With the book（PB p74）*Listen. Point. Trace.*** 播放角色故事视频，每场景暂停问 *Where's the cat?* 用 L1 问 *What's happening?* 给每对儿童一件软玩具（cat, dog, rabbit, mouse 等）；轮流把玩具放椅子上/下、桌子、问 *Where's the (rabbit)?* 巡视听并帮助。播音频儿童听；*Listen and point.* 再播儿童听指每幅图中的猫。*Say Trace.* 儿童描每幅图的猫。
**Track 49（录音文本，含梦境引子）：** *[Dream sequence introduction]  Pop: Look, a cat!  Pippa: A cat! Where's the cat?  Pop: It's under the table.  Pop: Tum-te-tum-te-tum. Oh, no! It's the cat!  Pippa: Where's the cat now?  Pop: It's under the chair.  Pippa: Where's the cat now?  Pop: It's ON the chair.  Pippa: Pop… Where's the cat now?  Pop: Ohhh, errr… It's here and… It's BIG!  Pop: Hmmmm.*
**After the book：** 把软玩具放桌上问如 *Where's the dog?*（It's on the table.）移到桌下重复。
**Activity Book（p74）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 提醒儿童找每行「odd one out」，先一起做第一行示例；儿童用指或笔圈出 odd one out（认不同图比画完美圆圈重要）；描完后指 odd one out 说词或短语（chair, table, under the chair, under the table）。
**Learning to Learn：** 展示 table, chair, on, under 闪卡让儿童命名，命名后用 TB 第 7 页一种技巧做自我评估。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice with a chant）—— Pupil's Book page 75

**Objective：** Practise Where's…?, on and under with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** chair, table, on, under.
**Language：** **It's (under) the (chair).**
**Materials：** items for an obstacle race (e.g. blankets, tape, beanbags, balls, buckets); flashcards dog, fish, cat, rabbit; soft toy animals (optional); colouring pencils or crayons; Unit 6 chant video; toys (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；如可能设置障碍课程（毯子下钻、走线、头顶平衡豆袋），用英中讲解（*Go under the blanket! Walk on the line. Pick up the beanbag. Put it on your head. Run and put the ball on the bucket.*）；儿童轮流完成，尽量用英语鼓励；或用软玩具宠物让儿童轻轻玩并喂食物水。
**Before the book：** 把宠物闪卡或软玩具动物放不同位置（椅子/桌子/书上或下面），问如 *Where's the (cat)?*（It's under the chair.）
**With the book（PB p75）*Listen. Colour. Sing.*** 随意问宠物，如 *Where's the fish?* 儿童指正确图，用正确介词造句，如 *It's on the table.* 播放 **Where's the cat? chant** 儿童听。再播每段后暂停让儿童指并涂宠物；查问如 *Where's the dog?*（Under the chair.）再播让儿童跟上、举起宠物闪卡作提示。
**Track 50 Where's the cat? chant：** *Adult: Where's the dog? Where's the dog?  Child: It's under the chair! It's under the chair! Look!  Adult: Oh, yes!  Adult: Where's the fish? Where's the fish?  Child: It's on the table! It's on the table! Look!  Adult: Oh, yes!  Adult: Where's the cat?…  Child: It's on the chair!…  Adult: Where's the rabbit?…  Child: It's under the table!…*
**After the book：** 再播 chant 儿童能做动物动作处跟上（on：握拳放平掌上；under：握拳放平手掌下）；可把软玩具或闪卡放在与 chant 相同位置作提示；结束时播放 Where's the cat? chant 视频鼓励跟上。
**Learning through play — A memory game：** 把软玩具动物和/或玩具放教室各处桌椅上下；志愿者上前看房间一分钟转身，问如 *Where's the teddy?* 儿童不回头记忆回答如 On the table，答对得一分；全班轮流问问题。
**Activity Book（p75）：** *Look. Find. Circle. Say.* 儿童看色键每个剪影，在大图中找动物并圈出；完成后你说真/假句子，如 *The cat. It's on the table. The rabbit. It's under the chair.* 儿童起身代表 yes、坐下代表 no；再问全班 *Where's the cat?*（It's on the chair.）等；儿童结对问与答 Where's the…?
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers 数字）—— Pupil's Book page 76

**Objective：** Practise counting using numbers 1–8.
**Vocabulary：** numbers 7, 8.
**Language：** **(Seven) (rabbits).**
**Materials：** large piece of paper with six large circles numbered 1–6; known small items for the children to count (e.g. one teddy, two small dolls, three crayons, four balls, five pencils, six toy cars); puppet; eight known objects (e.g. trains); red and blue colouring pencils or crayons; two rolled-up newspapers; water tray; eight plastic fish or ducks with a number written on the bottom 1–8.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在地面铺一张有六个编号圆圈的大纸，旁边放一堆物品。喊如 *Three crayons.* 请一志愿者数出蜡笔放入 3 号圈；重复直到所有物品都放对圈。和全班再数（*One! One teddy. One, two! Two dolls.*）盖住纸，看儿童能否记住每个圈有什么（*One?* One teddy!）
**Before the book：** 在板上写数字 1–6。儿童唤出 Pippa，Pippa 指数字数 *One, two, three, four, five, six!* 儿童跟数；Pippa 指不同数字点儿童说。举起不同数量物品（如六支小玩具火车）一起数 *One, two, three, four, five, six. Six trains.* 加一件数到 7，写 7；以同样方式呈现 8。
**With the book（PB p76）*Listen. Count. Colour.*** 与全班指图一起大声数狗。*Listen and copy.* 播音频儿童拍手；再播全班跟上指动物数。儿童沿箭头用指或铅笔描数字（匹配框的颜色涂红或蓝），描与上方数字匹配的那组狗。问 *Where are the rabbits? Where are the cats?* 儿童指图。
**Track 51（录音文本）：** *One, two, three, four, five, six, seven. Seven rabbits.（拍手七下）One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight. Eight cats.（拍手八下）*
**After the book：** 在板上写数字 1–8。给两个志愿者各一卷报纸；喊一个数字，儿童先轻轻打到正确数字者胜（出示数字并说出）；换儿童重复。
**Learning through play — Water play with numbers：** 准备水池，放塑料鱼或橡皮鸭，如可能用永久记号笔在底部写 1–8 的数字（数字在水面下隐藏），或用数字标签和胶带。儿童轮流选一条鱼/鸭、出示数字并说出。
**Activity Book（p76）：** *Look. Count. Trace. Say.* 与全班指图数狗；儿童数每盒物品并涂正确数字的圈（先一起做第一个示例），再示范用黄笔描数字 5、描 5 与三明治之间的线；其余食物和宠物同样描；结对练习谈说（如 Six rabbits）。
**At home：** 让儿童在家找一种本单元提到的玩具动物，再数出七八件玩具练习计数。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Where's the cat? chant（Track 50）让儿童跟上；Pippa 表扬全班表现。

---

### Lesson 10（Science：What animals need）—— Pupil's Book page 77

**Objective：** Talk about what animals need.
**Vocabulary：** animals, food, home, toy, water.
**Language：** **Animals need (water).**
**Materials：** flashcards rabbit, cat, dog, fish; pictures of different pets and wild animals, one per child or pair of children (optional); colouring pencils or crayons; modelling clay or felt; buttons; plastic eyes; pipe cleaners; feathers; glue; phone/tablet (optional).
**Skills：** Social Responsibilities: Understanding and discussing global issues—environmental, political, financial and social: Identifies basic needs in human life (e.g. a home, a family, clean water etc.).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 Where's the cat? chant（Track 50）儿童跟上做动作。
**Before the book：** 展示宠物闪卡，用英中问 *What do animals need?* 儿童输入单元间谈过的动物和自己养的宠物，提出建议，改述成英语（food, water, a home, exercise）；欢迎并表扬所有想法。
**With the book（PB p77）*Look. Trace.*** 指页顶照片说 *Look! A beautiful rabbit.* 用英中问 *What does it need?* 识别（water, food, a home）；儿童描他们认为兔子真需要的物品。检查想法，让儿童解释选择（可能争议兔子是否需水）；问所有动物都需要什么，可谈野生动物不同的家（树上的巢、地下洞、洞穴）。
**After the book：** 给儿童不同动物图（有些宠物、有些野生动物，每人/每对一张）；儿童思考图中动物需要什么，尽可能用英语/L1 说（water, food, trees, to be free）。
**Activity Book（p77）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 指圆图问 *What's this?*（A rabbit.）儿童看右边图并把兔子需要的东西框涂色；核对并用 L1 讨论（兔子不需要玩具）；可解释野生兔子成群住在大的兔子窝里，从露水和植物（草和花）取水。
**Learning through play — Make your own pet：** 提供黏土配纽扣、塑料眼睛、毛根、羽毛等；儿童做自己的宠物（不必是真实动物），按自己喜好装饰（或可用毛毡轮廓粘上眼睛/腿/羽毛）；边做边用英中问（*What's your pet's name? What food does it like? Where does it sleep? How many legs? What colour is it? Does it play with toys? Which toys?*）；让儿童小群自由玩宠物；儿童向全班展示宠物，说名字、动物类型、吃什么等。可录像稍后播放。
**Finishing the class：** 展示 rabbit 闪卡问儿童记得什么：*What do animals need?* 儿童提建议（water bowl, food bowl, hutch），在闪卡旁画图；讨论人类需哪些东西（是否与动物相同或不同）；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 78

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** pets.
**Materials：** Unit 6 flashcards; soft or plastic toy pets, teddies, dolls; countable food (or use flashcards); books (optional); colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 若空间允许，告诉儿童他们都是 mice，场地一侧（如一面墙）是家、那里安全；游戏从不是家的那侧开始。问 *Where's the cat?* 给一个儿童 cat 闪卡/玩具猫说 *Here it is!* 该儿童走到场地中央（在其他儿童和家之间）；说 *Three, two, one… Go!* 游戏开始，「猫」要在 mice 跑回家前抓它们；若 cat 在它们回家前碰到任何 mouse，该 mouse 下一轮变猫；继续直到只剩几名仍是 mouse 者，他们是赢家。
**Before the book：** 用闪卡游戏（见 TB 13 页）复习 pets 和 Where's the…? Here it is!，用闪卡或软玩具作提示。
**With the book（PB p78）*Look. Match. Say.*** 确保儿童明白要匹配每只宠物的两半。*Where's the cat?* 给一个儿童 cat 闪卡/玩具猫，说 *Here it is!* 从左右描线。*IN the book：* 指示例图问 *What's this?*（A dog.）儿童从做到右描线匹配；描完后逐指每种宠物说名字。
**After the book：** 选含单个物品图的一页（如 p37），问 *Where's the (doll)?* 儿童指出（或用板上的闪卡）。儿童围坐一圈，圈中央放已知物品的软/塑料玩具（pets, toys, countable food, a book, a crayon 等；或闪卡确保单数）；*Where's the (teddy)?* 儿童在中央找到举起说词并拿回座位，直到全部拿完。请儿童帮你收拾，他们向持物儿童说如 *The teddy, please* 收到说 *Thank you*。
**Activity Book（p78）：** *Look. Draw. Say.* 儿童画喜欢的动物（cat, dog, rabbit 或 fish）及所需物品；边画边用 L1 问 *Tell me about your picture.* 完成后点名一种宠物，画了该宠物的儿童起立展示，志愿者尽量用英语谈图（food, water, ball）。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Unit 6 开篇视频谈论所学；若空间小可玩 Where's the cat? chant（Track 50）跟唱做动作；播 **The animals song（Track 44）**结束。

---

### Lesson 12（Project）—— Pupil's Book page 79

**Objectives：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; Carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** pets.
**Language：** **Where's the (cat/dog)? It's (under/on) the (table/chair).**
**Materials：** Unit 6 flashcards; soft toy animals (optional); puppet; photocopiable cat and dog features, page 252 (one set per child); white or brown paper lunch bags or paper plates (one per child); glue; colouring pencils or crayons; pre-made unit objective picture cards; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放本单元最爱的歌，儿童唱歌做动作。
**Before the book：** 在教室各处放玩具或闪卡（有些在桌椅上下），确保其中一件是猫。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，Pippa 问不同儿童教室里东西在哪如 *Where's the rabbit? Where's the book? Where's the blue ball? Where's the fish?* 鼓励儿童答如 *It's under the table* 或 *Here it is*。最后让 Pippa 紧张地问 *Where's the cat?* 儿童说如 *It's under the table.* 让 Pippa 说 *Under the table?* 跑开躲起逗笑。开课前复印/打印并裁剪猫和狗的特征（每个儿童至少一套），在棕色纸袋上贴合适特征各做一个猫、狗玩偶；向儿童介绍玩偶让他们辨认。
**With the book（PB p79）*Look. Make. Say.*** 用照片展示做猫/狗的步骤；每个儿童选做猫或狗，鼓励说 *I like dogs* 或 *I like cats.* 发纸袋和对应特征；边涂边协助粘贴，鼓励命名特征（mouth, eyes, nose, ears）；可加装饰（棉球、毛线团、绒球、颜料）。
**After the book：** 儿童轮流把自己的猫/狗放椅子上/下并说如 *It's under the table.* 其余儿童可跟上。把完成的宠物做成教室展示，用纸做桌椅背景；儿童挑选猫/狗的位置并谈（*My cat! It's under the chair.*）*Learning to Learn*：举书快速翻过 Unit 6 说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 6.* 用图片卡谈儿童做过的事、问喜欢/不喜欢哪些活动；鼓励唱歌段、L1 复述故事、说自己养的宠物、动物需要什么。自我评估按 TB 40 页流程；儿童把 Unit 6 星星贴纸贴在右上角。*Activity Book（p79）：* *Point. Say. Colour.* 儿童说动物和描述每幅图的句子（Rabbit. It's under the table. Dog. It's under the chair. Cat. It's on the chair. Fish. It's on the table.）边巡视边听；错误时给时间自纠，必要用升调重复。*Finishing the class*：提醒在家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

## Units 4–6 Review

复习页：Pupil's Book pages 80–81（原书页 166–169），分两课（Language review、Language and numbers review）。

### Review Lesson 1 —— Language review（PB p80）

**Objective：** Review language from Units 4–6.
**Vocabulary：** food and drink, parts of the body, pets.
**Language：** **I like (apples). I don't like (bananas). Touch (his) (eyes). Where's the (cat)? It's (under) the (table).**
**Materials：** flashcards from Units 4–6; red, brown, blue and yellow colouring pencils or crayons; soft toy animals (optional); materials for children to make a collage of things they like (magazines, paper, scissors, glue).
**Starting the class：** 播放 **The body song（Track 27）**儿童跟唱做动作；把软玩具动物藏在教室各处练 Where's the (cat)? It's (on) the (chair).
**Before the book：** 把 Units 4–6 的混合闪卡放桌上或板上；选一种食物说如 *Food! Apples! Mmm, look at the food!* 把食物卡放一侧，请不同儿童以同样方式找出其他食物闪卡放一起，尽量多说关于物品的话；如此把闪卡分类为 food、parts of the body 和 pets。用闪卡游戏（TB 13 页）复习食物饮品；用「Simon says」游戏复习身体部位（说 *Simon says, point to your eyes.* 儿童应指眼睛；说 *Point to your nose.* 儿童不应做，因为你没说 Simon），用其他身体部位重复。
**With the book（PB p80）*Listen. Find. Point. Say.*** 儿童看大图，说 *Find the food. Point!* 再说如 *Point to the biscuits.* 儿童指出。播第一段音频儿童听指，暂停给时间找食物并说句子。再播第二段让儿童指认并说身体部位。*Listen, find and point.*（Track 52 边听：可边听暂停。）播最后一段。
**Track 52（录音文本）：** *I like apples. Point to the apples. Say 'I like apples'. Point to the bananas. Say 'I don't like bananas'. Point to the sandwiches. Say 'I don't like sandwiches'. Point to the biscuits. Say 'I like biscuits'. Look at the children. Look at the boy. Touch his eyes. Say 'eyes'. Touch his nose. Say 'nose'. Touch his legs. Say 'legs'. Touch his feet. Say 'feet'. Look at the girl. Touch her ears. Say 'ears'. Touch her mouth. Say 'mouth'. Touch her arms. Say 'arms'. Touch her hands. Say 'hands'. Can you find the animals? Where's the cat? It's under the table! Point to the cat. Say 'It's a cat.' Where's the rabbit? It's under the chair! Point to the rabbit. Say 'It's a rabbit.' Where's the dog? It's with the man! Point to the dog. Say 'It's a dog.' Well done!*
**After the book：** 问更多大图内容，如 *Where's the juice? What colour is the dog? Let's count the bananas.* 给儿童两分钟记住图，然后 *Close your books!* 问更多问题可分组玩。
**Activity Book（p80）：** *Look. Find. Colour. Say.* 指页底物品问 *Where's the (juice)? What colour is it?*（Red.）儿童在大图找物品用同色涂；涂后结对谈图。
**Learning through play — A 'things I like' collage：** 儿童剪下自己喜欢的东西做拼贴；个别展示完成拼贴谈，如 *I like cats. I like pizza. I like dolls.* 需要时补词。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Units 4–6 中儿童最喜欢的歌，跟上做动作。

### Review Lesson 2 —— Language and numbers review（PB p81）

**Objective：** Review language from Units 4–6 and counting.
**Vocabulary：** food and drink, parts of the body, pets, numbers 1–8, big, small.
**Language：** **(Six) (rabbits).**
**Materials：** whistle; toys/items of food for counting (optional); pre-made number cards 1–8 (optional); yellow, brown, blue and red colouring pencils or crayons; pegs or hula hoops and beanbags (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；儿童在教室移动（或大空间跑动），吹哨停；说如 *Jump like a rabbit!* 儿童照做直到再次吹哨；用不同指令重复，示范每个动作/声音（Hop on one leg. Wave your arms. Walk like a cat. Touch your nose. Bark like a dog. Clap your hands. Eat a banana. Swim like a fish. Stamp your feet.）
**Before the book：** 把预制数字卡 1–8 放桌上；举起几支夹子并指一张数字卡，如 4，数出正确数量夹子、装到卡上；换不同卡重复。叫志愿者上前选一张数字卡、数出夹子装上；或给结对儿童两三张数字卡和夹子自己完成。*Alternatively*：地面上放八个呼啦圈各放一张数字卡；志愿者站在约一米外，喊一个数字，儿童把豆袋扔进正确圈，轮流。
**With the book（PB p81）*Look. Count. Colour.*** 指数字儿童说词（five, six, seven, eight）；指图中动物/食物组问 *What colour?* 儿童数每组物品并涂正确数字圈（先一起做第一个示例）。示范用黄笔描数字 5、再描 5 与其匹配三明治之间的线；其余食物和宠物同样做；结对练习谈说（如 Six rabbits）。
**Activity Book（p81）：** *Look. Count. Trace. Say.* 举书指三明治数（*One, two… three, four, five!*）帮儿童数每盒物品并涂正确数字圈（先做示例）；再用铅笔描数字并连线（先用黄笔描 5 和线）。
**After the book：** 一起数教室物品，如 *Let's count the books on my table. One, two, three, four, five, six! Six books.* 在板上写数字 5–8，指数字儿童说（先按序再随机）。在教室前摆玩具、蜡笔、铅笔、苹果、香蕉等组；请儿童上前用英语数并指板上正确数字。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Units 4–6 中儿童最喜欢的游戏；用下课常规结束。

---

## Unit 7 Clothes

单元主题：Clothes（衣服）。Pupil's Book 页 82–93（原书页 171–193）。

### 单元目标（Unit objectives）

- 熟悉服装词汇：**hat, jacket, shoes, socks, dress, skirt, trousers, T-shirt**。
- 学会听指令穿脱衣物：**Put on/Take off your (hat).** 并表达拥有：**I've got (a dress).**
- 学习字母读音 **i**（the letter sound i，以 insect 为例）。
- 学习形状：**circle（圆）、square（方）、triangle（三角）**。
- 跨学科（Art）：**rough and smooth（粗糙与光滑）**。
- 故事价值：**Be considerate（体贴他人）**；阅读故事 *Tommy's T-shirt*。
- 复习与进度评估。

---

### Lesson 1（单元开篇歌曲）—— Pupil's Book page 82

**Objective：** Become familiar with the topic of clothes and listen to a song.
**Vocabulary：** hat, jacket, shoes, socks.
**Materials：** real clothes and doll's clothes (including shoes, socks, hats and jackets); pre-made unit objective picture cards; puppet; Unit 7 introduction video; flashcards hat, jacket, shoes, socks; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在教室布置「换装区」，摆放各种衣物（鞋、袜、帽、夹克等），儿童自由选择穿戴玩耍；边玩边用英语交谈，如 *Who are you? Are you Daddy? What colour is this shirt? Your hat is big!*
**Before the book：** 与全班过一遍单元目标（见 TB 19 页，改适 Unit 7）。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，让她看到所有衣物很兴奋说 *Clothes!* 让 Pippa 穿戴上一些衣物（或玩偶衣），Pippa 指所穿之物说词，或背起/坐在大号帽子、夹克、鞋袜上。重复衣物词汇，鼓励儿童点指身上所穿之物。播放 Unit 7 视频引入主题，暂停问儿童看到什么、以为本单元学什么。
**With the book（PB p82）*Listen to the song.*** 指中央照片说 *Look! They're going out to play. They've got hats.* 用同样方式谈论其他照片，如 *He's going to play. He's putting on his jacket. A green jacket. / She's going out to play. She's got pink shoes.* 问儿童若要出去玩耍会穿什么；天冷/热/下雨时会穿什么。指页角 Pop 和 Pippa 的图，说 *Song time! Listen!* 播放 **The clothes song** 让儿童听，如提示举出或展示闪卡。再播儿童指点相关衣物。帮儿童找到 Unit 7 贴纸（jacket 和 socks）贴到正确位置。
**Track 53 The clothes song（录音文本）：** *Pippa: Let's sing about clothes!  Pop: Good idea, Pippa!  (Shows socks and shoes flashcards.)  Child 1: Put on your socks. Put on your shoes. Come on, come on and play.  (Show hat and jacket flashcards.)  Child 2: Put on your hat. Put on your jacket. Come on, come on and play.  All: Play, play, play, play, play, play, play.  (Puts socks and shoes flashcards behind your back.)  Mum: Take off your shoes. Take off your socks. Come on, come on and wash.  (Put hat and jacket flashcards behind your back.)  Mum: Take off your hat. Take off your jacket. Come on, come on and wash.  All: Wash, wash, wash, wash, wash, wash, wash.*
**After the book：** 再播歌带动作（穿衣服、再脱下），儿童可为「play」四处移动、为「wash」模仿洗脸。*Finishing the class*：Pippa 说词时收走衣物，用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 2（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 83

**Objective：** Name items of clothing and follow instructions (Put on/Take off).
**Vocabulary：** hat, jacket, shoes, socks.
**Language：** **(Put on/Take off) your (hat).**
**Materials：** flashcards hat, jacket, shoes, socks; clothes: real socks and shoes; a real hat and jacket (optional); colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The clothes song（Track 53）**儿童跟唱做动作。
**Before the book：** 展示闪卡，逐一指并说词数遍，儿童重复（或用实物教词）。叫一志愿者上前，说一个衣物词，儿童指正确闪卡或拿起实物；用不同志愿者重复。把闪卡/衣物发给不同儿童，每个儿童说词再传给下一个人，重复。叫一志愿者上前戴帽（如有可用其自己的帽），说 *Put on your hat.* 用手势表示意义，重复，儿童戴上帽。再示范 *Take off your hat.*（手势）儿童脱下帽。换不同志愿者呈现 *Put on/Take off your jacket.* 给出 Put on/Take off 指令（如 *Put on your socks.*）儿童以动作回应。
**With the book（PB p83）*Listen. Point. Colour.*** 带儿童念出音频词若干遍，儿童重复。播放音频让儿童听；再播边听边指点衣物。再播，暂停让儿童给衣物涂色。然后 Say *Point to the (jacket).* 儿童指出每个衣物。请志愿者说所用颜色。播放音频逐字核对并练听。
**Track 54（录音文本）：** *Mummy: Oh, brrr! Your jacket, Dan. Put on your jacket. Kim, your hat. Put on your hat!  [Clock strikes one]  Mummy: Oh, your socks. Take off your socks, Kim. Dan, your shoes! Take off your shoes, Dan.*
**Learning through play（song）：** 用 *Head, shoulders, knees and toes* 曲调唱下列歌，儿童在被提及时模仿穿戴动作，然后转身捏鼻子：*Hat, jacket, socks and shoes, / Socks and shoes.（x2）Turn around and touch your nose!*（再对袜/鞋重复。）
**After the book：** 展示闪卡，指图说词鼓励儿童跟上；用 Pippa 带自评。*Finishing the class*：用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 3（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 84

**Objective：** Practise following instructions.
**Vocabulary：** hat, jacket, shoes, socks.
**Language：** **(Put on/Take off) your (hat).**
**Materials：** real hats, jackets, shoes, socks (a set for each pair – optional); a clothesline strung across part of the classroom; pegs; flashcards hat, jacket, shoes, socks; Pupil's Book stickers.
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Learning to Learn: Practical skills for participating in learning: Follows instructions and class routines.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；唱 **The clothes song（Track 53）**儿童跟上做动作。
**Before the book：** 用夹子把真帽、夹克、袜子和鞋挂上晾衣绳（或用衣物闪卡挂上）。指并说名，儿童重复；再指让儿童离你独立说词。叫一志愿者，说如 *Put on your jacket.* 儿童指（你从绳上取下）正确物件并穿上（或模仿）；重复。练习 *Take off…* 并把物件挂回绳上。给全班下指令做动作（如 *Put on your hat.*）。
**With the book（PB p84）*Listen. Stick. Point. Say.*** 提醒儿童 Dan 和 Kim 想出去玩耍、得穿对衣服；用 L1 谈儿童要穿什么。问图画，如 *Where's Dan's jacket?* 儿童指，你说 *Yes! Here it is!* 帮儿童找到书后贴纸页；播音频儿童听指正确贴纸；再播暂停贴好。用随机顺序给出音频里的指令，儿童指点，核对是否能分辨不同图。儿童用指或铅笔圈出「odd one out」并检查；描完后结对，一人指出每排的 odd one out 并说短语（*Take off your hat. Put on your socks. Put on your shoes. Take off your jacket.*），另一人模仿动作，互换。
**Track 55（录音文本）：** *Dan, take off your shoes!  Kim, take off your socks!  Dan, put on your jacket!  Kim, put on your hat!*
**Activity Book（p84）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.*
**After the book：** 给每对儿童一顶帽、一件夹克、一双袜和一双鞋；一人给另一人指令（如 *Put on your/the socks.*）当儿童穿戴齐全，主导者说 *Take off your socks* 等。*Alternatively*：儿童传递一张衣物闪卡直到你说 *Stop!* 持卡儿童站起，一志愿者给含该词的指令（如 *Put on your hat!*），持卡儿童模仿。
**At home：** 让儿童在家试穿并说出衣物名称。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束（见 TB 17 页）。

---

### Lesson 4（Sounds and letters）—— Pupil's Book page 85

**Objective：** Practise recognising the letter sound i.
**S&L focus：** Practise letter sound i with the word insect.
**Materials：** pairs of socks made from white card and coloured in red, yellow, blue or brown – one sock per child; pre-made letter card i; brown colouring pencils or crayons; pictures of items or animals which start with the sound i (e.g. iguana, igloo, instrument, ink) and others which don't (e.g. ant, apple, doll, elephant, bananas, shoe, chair); sand or chalk (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；分发已知颜色的卡片袜子（每个儿童一只），儿童找到相同袜子配成对。然后下颜色指令，如 *Red socks. Climb like a cat!* 只有红袜子儿童做该动作，如 *Yellow socks. Put on your hat! Blue socks. Jump like a rabbit! Brown socks. Touch your ears!*（注：游戏后收走袜子卡，儿童可用来玩配对游戏 pelmanism。）
**Before the book：** 在板上写大号小写 'i' 说字母音 i，儿童重复练几遍；再指字母让儿童自己练。把预制字母卡 i 在班里传，说 *Stop and say!* 持卡儿童发音。把昆虫图放字母 'i' 旁，指说 insect 发 i 音；再指让儿童说词和音。然后指让儿童自己独立说词与音，重复。若进度合适，儿童用手指在沙盘书空或在地上用粉笔画字母 i。
**With the book（PB p85）*Listen. Trace. Colour. Say.*** *Listen and point!* 播音频儿童指字母或昆虫图，核对指对。若班级能描字形，用空中手指书空字母 i 的竖笔再点上方一点，再用板上字母示范如何描（先向下的竖笔、再上方加点）。儿童用指尖或棕蜡笔在页面描字母 i。儿童用描字母同样的颜色给昆虫涂色。边走动听儿童指字母和昆虫、发音或说词。
**Track 56（录音文本）：** *i i i — insect insect insect — i insect*
**After the book：** *Look and listen: 'i' like 'insect' or no?* 告诉儿童听到以 i 开头的词就像昆虫一样嗡嗡叫；展示一系列图，有的以 i 开头、有的不是，逐个说词夸大首音，儿童只为 i 开头的做动作。表扬认真听。
**Activity Book（p85）：** *Listen again. Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 再播音频，儿童描字母 i、标记上方一点并圈出以 i 开头的图（昆虫）。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 **The clothes song（Track 53）**儿童跟上做动作；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 5（Literacy：故事）—— Pupil's Book pages 86–87

**Objective：** Listen to, enjoy and answer questions about a story.
**Story language：** T-shirt, (very) big, small, new, nice, **Daddy has got an idea. Tommy is happy. Tommy isn't happy.**
**Materials：** dolls; cotton reels; lacing cards; basket weaving and other clothing-related toys (optional); flashcard T-shirt; real T-shirts (old, new, big and small); Big Book; Unit 7 literacy story video.
**Starting the class：** 摆放与服装相关的玩具供儿童玩耍。用讲故事常规安定课堂（见 TB 27 页）。
**Before the book：** 展示 T 恤或闪卡教 T-shirt。出示新旧两件 T 恤；举新 T 恤说 *This T-shirt is new. It's nice.* 举旧 T 恤说 *This T-shirt is old. It isn't so nice.* 重复 old 和 new。解释 nice 的意思。出示一大一小两件 T 恤复习 big 和 small。提醒儿童会听到一个家庭书店故事，打开 Big Book 让儿童找标题，检查对 Tommy's T-shirt 的理解。指图 1 的男孩说 *Look! This is Tommy. Look at his T-shirt. It's red. Can you see the teddy?* 指图 2 中 Tommy 的父亲问 *Who's this?*（Daddy.）
**With the book（PB p86–87）*Listen.*** 儿童说出每幅图里发生什么，你用英语转述，如 *The T-shirt is too small for Tommy. But he likes it a lot, yes. Daddy wants Tommy to have a new T-shirt.* 播音频儿童看 Big Book，逐帧指图。每幅后提问：*Does Tommy like the T-shirt?*（Yes.）*What does Daddy think?*（Tommy's T-shirt is very small.）*What's in the bag?*（A new T-shirt.）*How does Tommy feel?*（Sad because he likes the old T-shirt.）*What does Tommy's daddy do?*（Put the old T-shirt on the teddy.）*Is Tommy happy now?*（Yes.）*Why?*（Because he can keep the old T-shirt for Teddy.）问儿童是否认为 Daddy 有好主意。
**Track 57 —— Tommy's T-shirt（录音文本）：** *Uh-oh! Tommy's T-shirt is very small.  Tommy: I like my T-shirt.  Tommy puts on the T-shirt.  Daddy: Oh, Tommy, it's nice. But it's very small.  It's a new T-shirt. It's big.  Daddy: Here, Tommy. Take off your small T-shirt, please.  Tommy isn't happy.  Daddy: Tommy, the new T-shirt is nice.  Tommy: No, Daddy! Please! I like it.  Daddy: OK…  Daddy has got an idea.  Daddy: I know! Let's put the small T-shirt on Teddy.  Tommy: Thank you, Daddy!  Tommy is happy now.*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍故事，用录音语言、拟声与动作。用图检查儿童明白 Tommy 的爸爸有主意并复习 Thank you；或播放读写故事视频。请儿童说出画面中人物感觉如何、为何那样表现，鼓励用观察和生活经验建立联系（inference 与 empathy）。
**Activity Book（p86）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 指底部 Tommy 说 *Tommy's T-shirt is very small.* 指场景说 *Daddy has got an idea. He says 'Let's put the T-shirt on'… Tommy?*（No!）*Daddy?*（No!）*Teddy?*（Yes!）儿童描圈出 Teddy。
**Finishing the class：** 用 L1 问 *What was the problem with Tommy's old T-shirt? What did Daddy do?* 鼓励儿童用 L1 复述故事。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 86–87

**Objective：** Recognise the value of being considerate, from the story.
**Value：** **Be considerate（体贴他人）。**
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Social Responsibilities: Understanding personal responsibilities as part of a group and in society – including citizenship: Describes in basic terms positive behaviours in the school environment (e.g. helping others, picking up litter).
**Story language：** T-shirt, (very) big, small, new, nice, **Daddy has got an idea. Tommy is happy. Tommy isn't happy.**
**Materials：** a clothesline; pegs; baskets; hats, jackets, socks, shoes for each team; Big Book; magazine pictures of clothes; glue; colouring pencils or crayons; plain white T-shirts (one per child), permanent markers and surgical spirit or paper T-shirt templates (optional).
**Starting the class：** 在儿童身高处挂起晾衣绳。分成两队，给每队一篮相同数量的衣物。用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 一起观看 Unit 7 读写故事视频；用 L1 问 *Why did Tommy's daddy put the T-shirt on the teddy?* 谈论图中儿童如何体贴，如 *Look! This girl's sharing. She's passing the scissors. This boy is picking up paper. He's tidying up. Everyone's being quiet. They're thinking about other people.* 用 L1 复述。再播故事儿童听指。
**With the book（PB p86–87）*Listen.*** 用 L1 谈论 Tommy 的爸爸，以及故事如何体现他为 Tommy 的感受着想、想让他开心。出示 Big Book。儿童指出显示 Tommy 的爸爸体贴的图。用 L1 问儿童若有人难过，还可以做什么让他人感觉好的事。
**Learning through play（team game）：** 各队队员在篮子旁排好队等待。当你说 *Three, two, one. Go!* 每队第一个儿童取一件衣物跑到晾衣绳，用夹子挂起、说词，然后回队。下一个儿童再取一件。第一支把篮中所有衣物挂晾完的队获胜。鼓励儿童互相说 *Thank you* 以表体贴。
**After the book：** 分组合作做衣物拼贴，安排小任务（有的分派图片与胶水、有的发纸、有的收拾）；儿童也可在空间使用和轮流上体贴。巡视鼓励儿童谈论拼贴画，提供所需语言。
**Activity Book（p87）：** *Draw. Colour. Say.* 儿童画并涂色自己张开开门的样子，结对一人模仿开门、另一人假装穿过并说 *Thank you!* 巡视说如 *Yellow and blue. That's nice! I like your T-shirt. It's a cat!*
**At home：** 让儿童告诉家人他们如何体贴他人、以后如何更体贴。
**Finishing the class：** 儿童收拾课堂时，鼓励他们对他人体贴；用下课常规结束。*Learning through play（decorate a T-shirt）*：给每个儿童一件纯白 T 恤来装饰，可用永久记号笔画简单星星（T 恤里垫厚卡纸或塑料，避免图案透底），可在上面滴外科酒精让墨色晕开成烟花；或用纸 T 恤模板，告诉儿童他们是时尚设计师，可画任意多设计（记得 Tommy 的 T 恤上画了昆虫和蜥蜴，可展示儿童认识的动物闪卡帮忙出主意）。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 88

**Objective：** Name items of clothing and express possession, I've got (a dress).
**Vocabulary：** dress, skirt, trousers, T-shirt.
**Language：** **I've got (a dress).**
**Materials：** Unit 7 flashcards; puppet; real items of either adult-sized or doll's clothes (a T-shirt, skirt, trousers and a dress); Unit 7 character story video; a clothes shop role-play area, including real skirts, trousers, dresses and T-shirts, till, play money (optional); blue, yellow, brown and red colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The clothes song（Track 53）**儿童跟上做动作，展示闪卡或真衣物。
**Before the book：** 课前把小件玩偶衣放进小包给 Pippa。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa；Pippa 拿着包出来、看起来很满意，说 *Look! I've got some new clothes.* 一件件从包里拿出物品，同时 Pippa 说 *I've got a skirt… a T-shirt… a dress and trousers.* 再指新物品让 Pippa 再说一遍 *A skirt! A T-shirt.* 等，说 *Wow! I like your new clothes, Pippa.*（或不用木偶，用大人衣物装进购物袋并说明是你的：*I've got some new clothes* 等。）与全班练习说新衣物词，然后用 *I've got…* 谈论。
**With the book（PB p88）*Listen. Point. Trace.*** 播放音频让儿童听。*Listen and point.* 再播音频，儿童听指每幅图中的衣物。*Trace.* 说明儿童需描圈图中每个衣物，可用指或铅笔。播 *Track 58* 儿童边听边指点并描。
**Track 58（录音文本）：** *[Dream sequence introduction]  Pippa: Look, Pop. I've got a new dress. I like my new dress.  Pop: Yes. It's nice!  Pippa: Look, Pop. I've got new trousers and a red T-shirt.  Pop: Wow! Nice!  Pippa: Look, Pop. I've got a new skirt. I like my new skirt.  Pop: Oh yes. It's nice!  Pop: Look, Pippa. I've got a new hat.  Pippa: Oh… yes… um… it's nice and it's BIG.*
**After the book：** 在教室布置「服装店」（含收银机、玩具钱、购物袋等），儿童轮流当店主或顾客，鼓励用英语如 *A skirt, please. Here it is. I've got a skirt and a hat.*（或玩闪卡游戏 TB 13 页来练 I've got… 和新衣物词。）
**Activity Book（p88）：** *Look. Find. Colour. Say.* 提醒儿童圆点可帮他们判断用什么颜色；巡视检查握笔，如 *What's this?*（A T-shirt.）*What are these?*（Trousers.）*What's yellow?*（Skirt.）*What colour is the dress?*（Brown.）儿童举手举书、指认衣物名称。
**Finishing the class：** 播放角色故事视频，每场景停让儿童补全句子（如 Pippa says 'I've got a new…'）；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 89

**Objective：** Practise I've got… and items of clothing with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** dress, skirt, trousers, T-shirt.
**Language：** **I've got (a dress).**
**Materials：** items of clothing from the unit vocabulary; Unit 7 flashcards; colouring pencils or crayons; Unit 7 chant video; a bag or suitcase with clothes and accessories for a summer holiday; bags or paper with suitcase outlines (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在换装区摆好衣物。告诉儿童准备一场「时装秀」；儿童可结对，一人是时装设计师决定另一人穿什么。巡视鼓励儿童说出衣物和颜色，如 *I've got a blue hat.* 等大家准备好，在教室内或大空间组织一条「走道」，点评造型，如 *Here we have Alvaro. Can you see his nice trousers? And the new red T-shirt. I like the jacket.*
**Before the book：** 儿童围圈坐。把所有衣物闪卡发下去。放音乐，儿童传递闪卡。音乐停，持卡儿童说如 *I've got shoes.* 直到每人都至少说到一次才重复；再播儿童可每一段后指相应图，并给每段对应衣物涂色。
**With the book（PB p89）*Listen. Colour. Sing.*** 观看 **The clothes chant** 视频，鼓励儿童跟上。问图中衣物，如 *Where are the trousers?* 儿童指；然后指图让儿童说 I've got… 句子。
**Track 59 The clothes chant（录音文本）：** *Pippa: I've got a skirt, I've got a skirt! Look, I've got a skirt!  Pippa: I've got a dress, I've got a dress! Look, I've got a dress!  Pop: I've got trousers, I've got trousers! Look, I've got trousers!  Pop: I've got a T-shirt, I've got a T-shirt! Look, I've got a T-shirt!  [Repeat]*
**Activity Book（p89）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童沿连线把儿童与衣服匹配；描完后用 I've got… 说句子（如 *I've got trousers.*），儿童指向正确的人。
**Learning through play（packing）：** 用一个装暑假衣物（含鞋、裙、T 恤等）的包或行李箱，出示说 *I'm going on holiday! This is my bag. What's in the bag?* 用 L1 必要时解释。打开包、举起一件说如 *I've got a red dress.* 鼓励儿童猜测其他物品（如 *A T-shirt! Socks!*）；若儿童用 L1 猜测，提供英文（如 sunglasses, a swimming costume）。给儿童包和衣物玩打包游戏；或用带行李箱轮廓的纸让儿童画或贴想带的东西。儿童讲述各自的包/图（如 *I've got a yellow skirt. I've got blue trousers.*）。
**After the book：** 握书再唱，出示相关闪卡提示；儿童可再chant并指自己穿的衣物或模仿穿衣服，*Starting the class*～可让儿童用自评技巧表达表演感受（见 TB 7 页 Learning to Learn 部分）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers / Shapes）—— Pupil's Book page 90

**Objective：** Practise recognising shapes (including squares, circles and triangles).
**Vocabulary：** colours, parts of the body, shapes.
**Language：** **It's a (circle/square/triangle).**
**Materials：** modelling clay and shaped cutters; pictures; shapes made of card: yellow square, blue circle, red triangle; yellow, blue and red colouring pencils or crayons; pre-cut paper or card squares, circles and triangles for the children to make; glue; phone/tablet (optional); clothesline; pegs.
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Critical Thinking: Understanding and analysing links between ideas: Sorts, arranges and describes objects by shape, size, colour, weight, texture and position.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；提供不同形状的彩泥和切模（含圆形、三角、方形），巡视鼓励儿童用 L1 想出形状名称。
**Before the book：** 出示黄色方形说 *A square.* 儿童重复；若可能方形在班里传、每人都说 Square。再出示蓝色圆形和红色三角形，练习说形状。指教室里这些形状的物件说如 *It's a circle!* 举方形问 *What colour is it?*（Yellow.）再用圆和三角形重复。
**With the book（PB p90）*Look. Colour.*** 在板上按 PB 键区顺序展示（或画）形状（square, circle, triangle）。指 PB 键区中的方形问 *What colour is it?*（Yellow.）说 *Colour the square yellow.* 儿童找黄色蜡笔、把大图中的方形涂黄，巡视检查。蓝色圆和红色三角形同样做。涂完后问如 *Where is the yellow square?* 儿童指图中方形；也可问如 *What colour are the circles?*（Blue.）*What shape are the eyes and ears?*（Circles.）
**Learning through play（shape game）：** 把卡片形状混放，志愿者按你说的顺序把它们挂上晾衣绳，如 *Marta, put on the triangle, square and circle.* 全班核对，然后一起大声说形状。再下指令脱下，如 *Tom, take off the circle. Cal, take off the square.*
**After the book：** 分发小张预制纸/卡形状，儿童做自己的图（像 PB 里的小老鼠）；图可抽象，也可做方形车厢+圆形轮子的火车，或人脸（三角鼻、方形嘴、圆形眼）。巡视问如 *What's this?*（A triangle.）*What colour is the circle?*（Blue.）请志愿者向全班展示成品并说形状名，儿童或想说他们做了什么（如 a toy 或 an animal）；可拍摄此活动稍后播放。
**Activity Book（p90）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 播放 **The clothes chant（Track 59）**让儿童跟上；儿童用指或铅笔描形状，完成后举书一起说 *Triangle, square, circle.* 也可乱序说名、儿童指点。
**At home：** 让儿童在家找三角、方形和圆的例子，可带实物或照片到下次课。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 10（Art）—— Pupil's Book page 91

**Objective：** Learn about Art: rough and smooth textures.
**Vocabulary：** rough, smooth.
**Language：** **It's (rough/smooth).**
**Materials：** puppet; rough or smooth objects, e.g. two boxes/bags, ridged and smooth shells, sandpaper, a pineapple, apples, fabric with different textures (optional); red and blue colouring pencils or crayons; scarf for a blindfold; items to review shapes (wooden/plastic shapes, printed outlines of shapes, scissors, mini whiteboards – optional); pasta shapes; card and glue.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；提供一批形状玩具（圆、方、三角），儿童用木制形状做人脸/图画/重复图案，并练习从纸或薄卡上用剪刀剪简单形状。把一件粗糙、一件光滑的物件放在粉笔槽上，如可能；指粗糙物件问 *Is it rough?* 儿童说 *Yes.* 在上面画一波浪线；光滑物件同样做画直线。解释波浪线代表粗糙物件、直线代表光滑物件。
**Before the book：** 举起一件光滑物件（如光滑贝壳）摸它说 *It's smooth. Smooth.* 儿童（及更多光滑物件）传递接触，每摸一件就跟着说 smooth。*Alternatively*：儿童轮流上前放对位置并说 rough 或 smooth。再收集光滑物件，换成粗糙物件（如有棱贝壳或菠萝）示范。把本次课物件分成两盒/两袋（一个放光滑、一个放粗糙），帮儿童分类。在书桌一头放光滑物件、另一头放粗糙物件，指说 *Smooth. Rough.* 把其余带来的物件堆成一堆。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa；Pippa 从堆里拿起一件，儿童喊 Rough 或 Smooth，Pippa 把物件放到书桌正确一端。
**With the book（PB p91）*Look. Trace.*** 指图问 *Rough or smooth?* 儿童描图下方线条、同时思考质地，巡视检查。接着志愿者举书、指向一件并说 rough 或 smooth。鼓励儿童摸图的线条感，说 *Rough or Smooth.*
**Learning through play（pasta pictures）：** 提供不同形状的意大利面条/通心粉让儿童做图；可在卡上画轮廓让儿童填，如人脸（儿童用通心粉贴眼睛、鼻子、嘴和头发）、动物或恐龙。
**After the book：** 叫一志愿者上前蒙住眼睛；把一件粗糙或光滑物件递给他们问 *Rough or smooth?* 儿童说词并试着猜是什么（用 L1）。换不同志愿者重复；儿童也可结对玩。
**Activity Book（p91）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 儿童看并把粗糙物件的边框涂红、光滑物件的边框涂蓝；检查答案并让儿童想更多粗糙/光滑质地的东西。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。加一句可 `[?]`：可让儿童数方形和三角形的边数（A square has four sides. A triangle has three sides.），并在板或迷你白板上画形状命名。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 92

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** clothes.
**Language：** **I've got (trousers/a T-shirt/a jacket/socks/a dress/a hat/a skirt/shoes).**
**Materials：** real clothing (trousers, T-shirts, jackets, socks, dresses, hats, skirts, shoes); tape; Unit 7 flashcards; colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；如可能清出教室空间。在一端放一堆衣物。儿童站在另一端起跑线（如一截胶带）后。当你说 *Three, two, one. Put on some clothes!* 儿童跑向衣堆、穿上一件、再跑回起跑线。设计时器，看五分钟内能穿上多少件衣服。儿童可分队玩：第一队成员回起跑线后，下一名出发，如此类推，时间到穿得最多的队获胜。（或玩 'Simon says'，用本单元所有衣物。）
**Before the book：** 用闪卡或真衣物复习衣物词。
**With the book（PB p92）*Look. Listen. Match. Say.*** 确保儿童明白要每人匹配右侧一件衣物。指儿童拿着的和右侧的物件问 *What's this? / What are these?* 播音频，儿童用指或铅笔从左到右描线。匹配完成后，再播音频每句后暂停，儿童指图并重复。儿童可在完成后做更大的轮廓图，自画拿着本单元（发散自选）衣物，把衣物闪卡当提示摆在板上、并在板上画示例轮廓。
