**Track 60（录音文本）：** *Girl 1: I've got trousers and a T-shirt. And I've got a jacket!  Boy 1: I've got trousers and a T-shirt. And I've got socks!  Girl 2: I've got a dress and a hat. And I've got a skirt!  Boy 2: I've got socks and shoes. And I've got a T-shirt!*
**Activity Book（p92）：** *Look. Draw. Say.* 指示例图，引出男孩拿着袜子。说明儿童可画自己拿着所选衣物完成大轮廓，儿童画并涂色。巡视说 *Tell me about your picture.* 个别儿童说衣物名并说 I've got…；如孩子愿意，可给画像标注衣物词，并与全班或结对分享。
**After the book：** 八名志愿者排成一列，每人一张衣物闪卡。告诉全班仔细看和听。第一个儿童向全班出示闪卡、说如 *I've got socks.* 下一个照做（给语言帮助）。给全班两分钟记住谁拿哪张卡，然后持卡者把卡藏到背后。无卡儿童轮流指一个持卡儿童并说正确衣物词；若说对，持卡者把卡再给全班看、重复其句子（I've got…），然后把卡交给你、退回原位。继续直到全班猜出所有卡。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Unit 7 引入视频，谈论看到什么、本单元学到什么；播放 **The clothes chant（Track 59）**结束。

---

### Lesson 12（Project 项目课时）—— Pupil's Book page 93

**Objective：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** clothes.
**Language：** **(Put on/Take off) your (jacket/hat/socks/shoes/dress/trousers/T-shirt/skirt).**
**Materials：** photocopiable dolls and clothes cut-outs, pages 253–254 (one set per child); scissors; pre-made unit objective picture cards; colouring pencils or crayons; Activity Book stickers; puppet (and doll's or real clothes for the puppet); Unit 7 flashcards; circles, triangles and squares made of card, large enough for a child to sit on (optional).
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Learning to Learn: Reflecting on and evaluating own learning success: Self-corrects from own stored knowledge when they realise a mistake has been made.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；如可能把卡片形状铺在教室地面或大空间（圆、三角、方形）。儿童跑动，你说如 *Sit on a triangle!* 儿童尽快找到三角形状坐下（可共坐）。若儿童不能移动，把三种形状分别贴在三面墙上，说如 *Point to the triangle!*
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa；如可能用玩偶裙和帽子给 Pippa 穿衣。告诉儿童仔细听，说 Yes 或 No；Pippa 说真真假假的句子让儿童判断，如 *I've got trousers.*（No.）*I've got a hat.*（Yes.）（或 Pippa 用购物袋装大人的衣物。）课前复印/打印玩偶模板并剪出人形（可也剪出夹克，儿童只需剪帽和鞋，或剪出所有衣物）。自己做一只剪贴玩偶给全班看。
**With the book（PB p93）*Look. Make. Say.*** 举书翻过 Unit 7，说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 7.* 若你在 Lesson 1 用图卡呈现过单元目标，指并谈论儿童做过的不同事；问他们喜欢/不喜欢哪些活动；请儿童跟唱部分歌曲、用 L1 复述故事、说形状、用 I've got… 谈论所穿衣物。然后说衣物词，儿童举起正确物品，评论如 *I like your blue hat. It's nice.* 个别儿童举起一件物品说如 *A hat* 或 *A blue hat.*
**Activity Book（p93）：** *Point. Say. Colour.* 儿童说图中儿童在说的话（*I've got a T-shirt. I've got trousers. I've got a skirt. I've got a dress.*）。发放剪贴玩偶和衣物让儿童自己剪（夹克可先穿好）；儿童剪纸/涂色时巡视问 *What's this? What colour are your shoes?* 等。发完衣物后给指令，如 *Put on your hat.* 儿童找正确物件给玩偶穿上；继续鞋、夹克。再用 Take off… 下指令。
**After the book：** 确保儿童手上有玩偶和衣物，可翻开玩偶参照。儿童可玩「装扮游戏」并描述。*Self-evaluation*：按 40 页的自评流程进行；儿童把 Unit 7 星级（startop）放好。若儿童犯错，给时间自我纠正；必要时重复错误并用疑问语调停顿等儿童自己改正。
**Finishing the class：** 提醒儿童回家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

## Unit 8 Transport

单元主题：Transport（交通工具）。Pupil's Book 页 94–105（原书页 195–217）。

### 单元目标（Unit objectives）

- 熟悉交通工具词汇：**bike, bus, car, van**。
- 学会谈论所见：**I can see (a car).** 并表达能力：**I can (ride a bike).**
- 认识并使用动作动词：**drive a car, jump, ride a bike, run**。
- 学习字母读音 **o**（the letter sound o，以 orange 为例）。
- 学习数字 **9 和 10**。
- 跨学科（Science）：**fast and slow（快与慢）**。
- 故事价值：**Be careful（小心谨慎）**；阅读故事 *The hare and the tortoise*。
- 复习与进度评估。

---

### Lesson 1（单元开篇歌曲）—— Pupil's Book page 94

**Objective：** Become familiar with the topic of transport and listen to a song.
**Vocabulary：** bike, bus, car, van.
**Materials：** toy vehicles, e.g. trains with tracks, toy cars, motorbikes, lorries, diggers; pictures of vehicles; pre-made unit objective picture cards; Unit 8 introduction video; flashcards bus, bike, car, van; Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；在教室不同区域摆放玩具交通工具（如小车放「公路」垫或赛车跑道、玩具挖掘机放沙区、小火车带轨道搭建），儿童自由选择玩耍；边玩边用英语交谈，如 *What colour is this car? Let's count the carriages of the train.*
**Before the book：** 与全班过一遍单元目标（见 TB 19 页，改适 Unit 8）。展示不同交通工具图片，鼓励儿童谈论（如他们乘坐过哪些、最喜欢或最常用哪些）。播放 Unit 8 视频引入主题，暂停问儿童看到什么、以为本单元学什么。
**With the book（PB p94）*Listen to the song.*** 指中央照片说 *Look at the boy. He's got a car. A red car. And the girl has got a bike.* 谈论其他照片中的交通工具，如 *I can see a yellow van. / I can see London! There's a big, red bus and lots of cars.* 指页角 Pop 和 Pippa 的图，说 *Song time! Listen!* 播放 **The transport song** 让儿童听，如提示举出或展示闪卡。再播儿童指点相关交通工具（car, van, bus, bike）。帮儿童找到 Unit 8 贴纸（car 和 bus）贴到正确位置。
**Track 61 The transport song（录音文本）：** *Pop: Let's sing about transport.  Pippa: OK!  Pop: A car! A car! I can see a car. It's a car. It's a car.  Pippa: A van! A van! I can see a van. It's a van. It's a van.  Pop: A bus! A bus! I can see a bus. It's a bus. It's a bus.  Pippa: A bike! A bike! I can see a bike. It's a bike. It's a bike.  It's a bike.*
**After the book：** 再播歌鼓励儿童跟上并加音效。玩 'Follow my leader'（见 TB 13 页）说不同交通工具（如 *Train!*）儿童像火车轮子一样绕臂并边走边发火车音效。*Finishing the class*：用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 2（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 95

**Objective：** Name vehicles and talk about what you can see.
**Vocabulary：** bike, bus, car, van.
**Language：** **I can see a (car).**
**Materials：** flashcards car, bus, bike, van; a toy car, bus, bike and van or pictures of vehicles (optional); puppet; colouring pencils or crayons; materials for junk modelling (e.g. boxes, tubes, card circles/squares, glue).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The transport song（Track 61）**儿童发音效、边开/骑每辆车动作边放音效，鼓励跟唱。
**Before the book：** 展示闪卡，逐一指并说词数遍，儿童重复（或用玩具车/公共汽车/自行车/小货车或图片）。玩发音游戏（见 TB 13 页）。叫一志愿者上前，说一个车辆名，儿童指正确闪卡或拿起玩具。用不同志愿者重复。把闪卡/玩具发给不同儿童，每个儿童说词再传给下一个人，重复。放一张闪卡上板（如 bike）；儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，Pippa 指板说 *I can see a…*，儿童接说 bike! 用不同闪卡重复。选一志愿者上前闭眼；举起一张闪卡（如 van）说 *I can see a…*，志愿者睁眼说 van! 用不同闪卡重复。
**With the book（PB p95）*Listen. Point. Colour.*** 指角色问 *Who's this?*（Kim, Dan, Mummy.）说 *They're outside their bookshop. What can they see?* 播放音频让儿童听。再播儿童听指车辆，核对指对。再播暂停让儿童按自己喜欢颜色涂车。涂完后说如 *Point to the car.* 鼓励儿童说所用颜色（如 A red car.）。
**Track 62（录音文本）：** *Dan: Look, a bus, a bus. I can see a bus!  Kim: Look, a bike, a bike. I can see a bike!  Kim: Look, a car, a car. I can see a car!  Dan: Look, a van, a van. I can see a van!*
**Learning through play（junk modelling）：** 提供材料让儿童做交通工具的废旧材料模型。巡视说如 *I can see a circle! I can see a car. Let's count the wheels.* 儿童展示做好的模型并说名称，给时间自由玩模型然后展示。
**Activity Book（p95）：** *Look. Find. Circle. Say.* 儿童在大图里找剪影所示的车辆、说出名称和颜色。指第一个剪影问 *What's this?*（Car.）*Look and find. How many cars?*（Two.）示范如何圈车并说 *I can see two cars.* 问 *What colour are the cars?*（Yellow and blue.）圈完其他车辆后，儿童结对指说（*I can see two buses. I can see a bike. I can see a van.*）。
**At home：** 让儿童外出散步，与家长/看护人谈论看到的车辆。
**After the book：** 开始在板上画一辆车，儿童猜，如 *I can see a bike.* 用其余车辆和其他物件（玩具、水果、动物）重复。
**Finishing the class：** 唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

### Lesson 3（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 96

**Objective：** Practise talking about what you can see.
**Vocabulary：** bike, bus, car, van.
**Language：** **I can see a (car).**
**Materials：** flashcards car, van, bike, bus and toy buses, cars, vans, bikes (one for each pair); Pupil's Book stickers; pictures with lots of known items to spot; video clip of vehicles and people on a street; junk model vehicles from Lesson 2.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；唱 **The transport song（Track 61）**鼓励儿童模仿并加音效。找一段城市街道视频片段（含已知可发现的物品：车、公共汽车、自行车、狗、鸟、男人或女人、帽子等）。*Ask What can you see?* 一次性播放视频；之后儿童说如 *I can see a car!* 看能造出多少不同句子。再播视频暂停确认或引出更多句子。*Alternatively*：若教室窗外有景观，儿童可向外看物。
**Before the book：** 逐渐露出 car 闪卡或慢慢从盒中拿出玩具车，同时说 *I can see a…?* 儿童说 car! 说 *I can see a car.* 儿童跟读句子。van、bike、bus 同样做。然后逐一举起车辆闪卡（或玩具）问 *What can you see?* 儿童答如 *I can see a car.*
**With the book（PB p96）*Listen. Stick. Point. Say.*** 帮儿童找到书后贴纸页；播音频儿童听指正确贴纸；再播暂停贴好。指图问 *What can you see?* 儿童说 *I can see a van* 并指点该如何沿右侧描图。儿童用指或铅笔描出匹配每张图的车辆。然后儿童指点描好的图说 *I can see a van. I can see a bus. I can see a car. I can see a bike.*
**Track 63（录音文本）：** *Mummy: Look, look. What can you see?  Dan: I can see a bus!  Daddy: What can you see? What can you see?  Kim: I can see a bike!  Daddy: What can you see?  Dan: I can see a car!  Mummy: What can you see?  Kim: I can see a van!*
**Activity Book（p96）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 指第一张图问 *What can you see?* 儿童说 *I can see a van* 并指出右侧该圈哪张图；依次。给每对儿童一辆玩具车，给时间玩。
**Learning through play：** 环视教室说如 *I can see a bus.* 持玩具公共汽车的儿童站起来并交回车辆；重复直到收齐所有车辆。
**After the book：** 展示一幅含人、动物、玩具、衣物和车辆的大图，问 *What can you see?*（如 *I can see a bike. I can see a cat. It's small.*）儿童看图两分钟并记住。盖住图，儿童回忆如 *I can see a car.*
**Finishing the class：** 用 Lesson 2 的废旧材料模型展示做自评，鼓励儿童结对或小组谈论模型；用问题帮他们反思所学（见 TB 7 页 Learning to Learn 部分）；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 4（Sounds and letters）—— Pupil's Book page 97

**Objective：** Practise recognising the letter sound o.
**S&L focus：** Practise letter sound o with the word orange.
**Materials：** cap or hat; bell; paper plate; recording of The wheels on the bus (optional); pre-made letter card o; orange colouring pencils or crayons; pictures of items or animals which start with the sound o (e.g. orange, ostrich, octopus, ox, otter, olive) and others which don't (e.g. apple, doll, elephant, igloo, bananas); modelling clay (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；把椅子排成两列或四列摆教室中央，像公共汽车座位，前面放一把司机椅。说 *Let's go on the bus!* 儿童模仿上车坐在椅子上，一名儿童当司机坐前面（戴帽、拿纸盘当方向盘）。儿童发引擎音效或听 The wheels on the bus；像巴士要到站一样摇铃，说 *Bus stop! Look out of the windows! What can you see?* 儿童停引擎声、发挥想象说如 *I can see a big dog! I can see a bike.* 给不同儿童当司机的机会；把铃交给不同志愿者选择何时「停公车」。
**Before the book：** 在板上写大号小写 'o' 说字母音 o，儿童重复；再指字母让儿童自己练。把预制字母卡 o 在班里传，说 *Stop and say!* 持卡儿童发音。把橙子图放字母 'o' 旁，指说 orange 并发音 o；再指让儿童说词和音，重复。
**With the book（PB p97）*Listen. Trace. Colour. Say.*** *Listen and point!* 播音频儿童指字母或橙子图，核实。若班级能描字形，用空中手指逆时针画圆书空字母 o，再用板上字母示范；儿童用指或橙蜡笔在页面描字母 o。让儿童给橙子上色；说明颜色名也叫 orange。边走动听儿童指字母和橙子、发音或说词。
**Track 64（录音文本）：** *o o o — orange orange — o orange*
**After the book：** *Look and listen: 'o' like 'orange' or no?* 告诉儿童听到以 o 开头的词就像采摘/剥橙子一样模仿；展示一系列图，有的以 o 开头、有的不是，逐个说词夸大首音，儿童只为 o 开头的做动作。
**Activity Book（p97）：** *Listen again. Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 再播音频，儿童描字母 o 并圈出以 o 开头的图（橙子）。若进度合适，儿童用彩泥做字母 o。
**Finishing the class：** 播放 **The transport song（Track 61）**儿童跳舞跟唱；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 5（Literacy：故事）—— Pupil's Book pages 98–99

**Objective：** Listen to, enjoy and ask questions about a story.
**Story language：** hare, tortoise, race, careful, fast, slow, win, well done.
**Materials：** ball (optional); pictures of a race, a tortoise and a hare (optional); Big Book; Unit 8 literacy story video.
**Starting the class：** 如可能组织一次赛跑，说 *Three, two, one. Go!* 开始。（或举行滚球比赛：说 *One, two, three. Go!* 儿童放球让它沿地滚，看谁的球滚得最远/先到终点，可分组进行。）用讲故事常规安定课堂（见 TB 27 页）。
**Before the book：** 用动作教 run 和 walk；儿童站起，你说 *Walk!* 原地走，说 *Run!* 原地跑。展示赛跑图，问谁喜欢参加或观看比赛。展示野兔和乌龟图教词。打开 Big Book 让儿童找标题。指图 1 的乌龟并模仿慢慢走，说 *Look! This is Tortoise. He's slow.* 儿童慢慢走并说 Slow。指野兔，模仿快速跑，说 *This is Hare. He's fast.* 儿童做动作并说 Fast。
**With the book（PB p98–99）*Listen.*** 儿童说出每幅图里发生什么，你用英语转述，如 *Hare and Tortoise are having a race. Hare is winning. He's fast. Tortoise is slow. Hare has fallen over. He can't run. Tortoise stops. He's helping.* 用 L1 解释 careful 的意思，检查儿童明白为何 Tortoise 赢而 Hare 输。播音频儿童看 Big Book，逐帧指图。每幅后提问：*Who's slow?*（Tortoise.）*Who's fast?*（Hare.）*What does Ostrich say to start the race?*（one, two, three. Go!）*Can Hare run?*（Yes.）*Who wins the race?*（Tortoise.）*What does Hare say?*（'Well done.'）解释 'Well done' 的意思。问 *Why is Hare happy?*（Because Tortoise helped him.）问儿童是否对谁赢赛跑感到意外。
**Track 65 —— The hare and the tortoise（录音文本）：** *Hare: Tortoise, you're slow. I'm fast!  Tortoise: Let's race!  Tortoise and Hare have a race.  Ostrich: One, two, three. Go!  Hare is fast.  Hare: I can run!  Tortoise is slow.  Tortoise: I can walk!  Tortoise is slow, but Tortoise is careful.  Tortoise: [humming]  Hare is fast, but Hare is not careful.  Hare: Ah!  Tortoise is slow, but he can walk. He can help Hare.  Tortoise wins the race!  Well done, Tortoise!*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍故事，用录音语言、拟声与动作；或播放读写故事视频。*Literacy development tip*：随班级更自信，把对话导向儿童认为故事的涵义、如何联系自己的生活；本单元可举何时小心比快更重要。
**Activity Book（p98）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 指小图说 *Listen and point.* 念故事里的句子（*One, two, three. Go! Tortoise is slow, but Tortoise is careful. Hare is fast, but Hare is not careful.*）儿童每句点正确图。说 *Who watches the race? Put your hand up* 并用手到眼示范 watch；儿童指猫鼬（meerkat），说 *Yes, the meerkat!* 看大场景问 *Who wins the race? Does Hare win?*（No.）*Does Tortoise win?*（Yes.）儿童描圈出 Tortoise。
**Finishing the class：** 问 *Who is slow/fast? Who is careful? Who wins?* 鼓励儿童用 L1 复述故事。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 98–99

**Objective：** Recognise the value of being careful, from the story.
**Value：** **Be careful（小心谨慎）。**
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Emotional Development: Empathy and relationship skills: Begins to show understanding of interactions that are hurtful or unfair to others (e.g. lying, ignoring, teasing, hitting, excluding etc.).
**Story language：** hare, tortoise, race, careful, fast, slow, win, well done.
**Materials：** items for an obstacle course (plastic cones, upturned buckets, etc.); paper plate for each child; beanbag (optional); Big Book; games for fine motor skills (e.g. threading beads, tweezers and small items to move, shapes to cut out, cotton buds and paint); colouring pencils and crayons; large card circles in red, green and yellow.
**Starting the class：** 设置障碍赛道或在沿地画粉笔路线（如可能用户外空间）。给每个儿童一张纸盘当方向盘。儿童沿赛道「开车」，避开障碍；鼓励发音效并小心驾驶，说 *Be careful!* 儿童可在小组完成赛道（若空间受限）。给指令改速度或交通工具，如 *Fast! Slow! Now drive a train/bus/van! Now ride a bike!*（或用两三张头顶豆袋运送的变体。）用讲故事常规安定课堂。
**Before the book：** 一起观看 Unit 8 读写故事视频，暂停问 *Who was careful?* 用 L1 谈论 Hare 不小时发生什么（他绊倒），给每天如何小心的例子（走路注意脚下、收拾好避免人绊倒、不碰倒东西）。问儿童在哪些地方尤其要小心（教室、商店、别人家、游泳池）。问何时慢慢小心做是好事（如涂色或剪纸、扣扣子、端饮料或收拾盘子杯子）。
**With the book（PB p98–99）*Listen.*** 用 L1 谈论图中没小心的人可能如何伤害/影响他人（男孩可能绊倒女孩、有人得捡/收拾女孩碰倒的积木），以及为何我们要小心（做好事、避免损坏物品或伤到他人/自己）。出示 Big Book，儿童指出显示 Tortoise 小心的图。再播故事儿童听指。用 L1 谈论图中儿童如何小心（男孩小心地拿包、女孩小心地走、男孩把购物放篮子里），问他们为何需要小心（包可能碰倒东西、女孩可能摔倒、男孩可能掉下购物）。
**Learning through play（traffic lights）：** 用绿、黄、红卡圆表示交通灯。边出示各卡边说 *Green is for fast! Yellow is for slow. Red is for stop!* 儿童走动，你出示绿卡说 *Green! Fast!* 他们走快；黄卡说 *Yellow! Slow!* 慢慢走；红卡说 *Red! Stop!* 静止不动。
**Activity Book（p99）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 儿童给图中小心的人涂色。
**After the book：** 设置需要儿童缓慢小心做的活动（用精细运动技能）。巡视表扬儿童小心，边工作边评论如 *Let's count. One, two, three, four! Be careful with the yellow paint. Is this a triangle or a circle?*
**At home：** 让儿童在家小心并和家人谈论。
**Finishing the class：** 儿童收拾时鼓励他们小心；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 100

**Objective：** Learn travel verbs and talk about ability.
**Vocabulary：** drive a car, jump, ride a bike, run.
**Language：** **I can (ride a bike).**
**Materials：** Unit 8 flashcards; puppet; Unit 8 character story video; yellow, red, brown, blue, pink and grey colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The transport song（Track 61）**儿童跟上做动作，需要时出示交通工具闪卡提示。
**Before the book：** 复习 run 并用闪卡和模仿/动作教 jump, ride a bike 和 drive a car。儿童唤出并问候 Pippa；Pippa 发指令让儿童做动作，变化顺序，如 *Drive a car! Jump! Run! Walk! Ride a bike!* 叫一志愿者做一个动作（需要时出示闪卡提示），全班说正确词/短语，如 *Ride a bike!* 用不同志愿者重复。*Say Look at me!* 跳起并说 *I can jump.* 儿童模仿动作和句子。run、ride a bike、drive a car 同样做。请志愿者到前面做其中一个动作，帮他们用 I can… 造句。
**With the book（PB p100）*Listen. Point. Trace.*** 播放音频让儿童听。*Listen and point.* 再播音频，儿童听指每幅图中做动作的人。*Trace.* 说明儿童需描图中做动作的人。播 *Track 66* 过程中儿童边听边指点描。念音频里的句子（如 *I can run*），儿童指正确的人。
**Track 66（录音文本）：** *Kim: Look at me! I can ride a bike.  Daddy: Well done, Kim!  Dan: Look at me! I can run.  Daddy: Well done, Dan!  Dan: Look at me! I can jump.  Daddy and Kim: Well done, Dan!  Daddy: Look at me! I can drive a car.  Kim and Dan: Well done, Daddy!*
**After the book：** 把动作闪卡（run, jump, drive a car, ride a bike）贴到四面墙。说含 I can… 的句子（如 *I can jump.*）儿童走到正确闪卡前做动作；若在座位，儿童可指卡再做动作。
**Activity Book（p100）：** *Look. Colour. Say.* 提醒儿童用彩色图判断黑与白图如何涂色，让两幅场景一致；巡视检查握笔，问颜色名及每个玩具在说什么（如 the cat says *I can drive a car.*）。图完成时，请儿童举书指点并一起说句子（*I can drive a car. I can jump. I can run. I can ride a bike.*）
**Finishing the class：** 播放角色故事视频，每场景停用 L1 问 *What's happening?* 复习 Well done! 的意思；让儿童补全句子（如 Kim says 'I can…'）。*Have Pippa* 指 drive a car, jump, run, ride a bike 闪卡让儿童说词并做动作；Pippa 可说 *Well done!* 并鼓掌。用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 101

**Objective：** Practise I can… with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** drive a car, jump, ride a bike, run.
**Language：** **I can (ride a bike).**
**Materials：** whistle (optional); flashcards drive a car, jump, ride a bike, run; toy car for Pippa (optional); puppet; Unit 8 chant video.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；儿童走动，你吹哨（或用拍手）时停。说动作让儿童模仿，如 *Ride a bike! Jump. Wave your arms. Drive a car. Touch your ears. Run. Clap your hands. Drive a train. Walk. Stamp your feet.* 用 *Fast! or Slow!* 变换节奏。
**Before the book：** 展示动词闪卡，儿童说词/短语并模仿。全班唤出并问候 Pippa；如可能开玩具车带 Pippa 出来，开过书本、课桌下等逗笑全班。Pippa 下车，说 *Wow! You're fast, Pippa! Well done!* Pippa 自豪地连说几遍 *I can drive!* 结对，一人当 Pop、另一人当 Pippa，再播 chant 各做自己角色的动作再互换。
**With the book（PB p101）*Listen. Trace. Sing.*** Pippa 看图说 *Look at Pop! Look at me! I can ride a bike! I can run!* 告诉儿童要学一支关于小老鼠的 chant。指 Pop 旁边的车图，示范如何沿连线到 Pop 开车的图，说 *Pop says 'I can…' (drive!)* 其余图同样做（省略最后词让儿童接）。播放 **The I can chant** 让儿童听。
**Track 67 The I can chant（录音文本）：** *(Show drive a car flashcard.)  Pop: I can drive, I can drive, I can drive a car!  (Show ride a bike flashcard.)  Pippa: I can ride, I can ride, I can ride a bike!  (Show jump flashcard.)  Pop: I can jump, I can jump, I can jump, look!  (Show run flashcard.)  Pippa: I can run, I can run, I can run, look!  [Repeat]*
**Activity Book（p101）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 儿童用指或铅笔沿箭头方向描右侧匹配左侧的图，巡视检查描线方向；然后念 I can… 句子（如 *I can drive a car.*），儿童指正确图。
**After the book：** 再播 chant，儿童跟上并模仿动作；逐个指右侧图让儿童说句子做动作（*I can jump. I can ride a bike. I can drive a car. I can run.*）
**At home：** 让儿童与家人谈论自己能做什么。
**Finishing the class：** 观看 **The I can chant** 视频，分成四组各做一动作，再播各组按时做动作；问反思问题鼓励自评（见 TB 7 页）。用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers 9, 10）—— Pupil's Book page 102

**Objective：** Practise counting using numbers 1–10.
**Vocabulary：** numbers 9, 10.
**Language：** **(Nine) (cars).**
**Materials：** puppet; yellow and red colouring pencils or crayons; pre-made number cards 1–10; ten bowls and groups of ten or more small objects to count (e.g. toy cars, pom-poms, buttons, pasta shapes, cereals); shoeboxes; small toy cars; number stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The I can chant（Track 67）**一半班级唱 Pippa 的句、另一半唱 Pop 的句。
**Before the book：** 把数字 1–8 写板上。儿童唤出 Pippa，Pippa 指数字并数 *One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight!* 儿童跟读；Pippa 指不同数字并选一名儿童说每个数。举起八件物品（如玩具车）一起数 *One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight! Eight cars.* 再加一件数到 9，把数字 9 写板上；同样方式呈现 10。在桌上放十个碗、各放一张 1–10 数字卡；每碗前放一堆小物件。儿童把正确数量物件放进每碗，边数出声（例如往 3 号碗放三件边数 *One, two, three!*）逐一核对碗和数字；再点每张数字卡让儿童说数、一起数每碗的物件。玩拍手游戏或唱歌练一到十（如 Ten green bottles）。
**With the book（PB p102）*Listen. Count. Colour.*** 与全班一起数图里的车。*Listen and copy.* 播音频让儿童拍手跟随；再播全班跟上、边数边指车辆。让儿童把 9 涂黄、10 涂红以匹配图框，可边涂边数。
**Track 68（录音文本）：** *One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine. Nine cars. (Clap nine times.)  One, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine, ten. Ten bikes. (Clap ten times.)*
**Activity Book（p102）：** *Look. Count. Trace. Say.* 儿童问 *Where are the cars? Where are the bikes?* 儿童用指或铅笔描数字，再从数字描线到车；完成后举书一起说 *Nine cars, ten cars.*
**Learning through play（car park）：** 用十个并排的鞋盒做停车场（前端剪掉），或在地板画十块区域，标记 1–10。在大量玩具车顶贴（1–10 的）数字贴纸，儿童必须把车停进数字区、按车上数字与停车场数字匹配；鼓励用英语大声说数字，也可说 *I can drive a car!* 全部停好后再和全班核对是否都在正确位置。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 10（Science）—— Pupil's Book page 103

**Objective：** Talk about speed (fast and slow).
**Vocabulary：** fast, slow.
**Language：** **It's (slow).**
**Materials：** red and blue colouring pencils or crayons; poster paint; thick paper; toy vehicles; ride-on toys (optional); pictures of fast and slow animals (e.g. tortoise, hare, snail, cheetah) and vehicles (e.g. hot air balloon, high-speed train, people walking, sports cars).
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Learning to Learn: Practical skills for participating in learning: Uses new language/knowledge while doing different tasks.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The I can chant（Track 67）**儿童唱歌做动作。（或用骑乘玩具户外自由玩：滑板车、车、工程车等。）
**Before the book：** 慢慢绕着教室走说 Slow，快速走说 Fast，重复。下指令让儿童执行，如 *ride a bike. Fast! ride a bike. Slow!* 示例动作：run a race、jump like a rabbit、drive a car、put on your shoes、drink juice. 展示一张慢动物/车辆图说 *It's slow.* 贴到板一端；展示快动物/车辆图说 *It's fast.* 贴到板另一端。举起更多快慢图，儿童帮你说 *It's fast/slow* 决定放板哪边；志愿者上前放对位置。（或提供户外的 ride-on toys 自由玩。）
**With the book（PB p103）*Look. Colour.*** 描述一张照片，如 *It's a car. It's slow.* 儿童指，其余照片同样做。把一件快、一件慢的物件放板上（如可能），每件旁画个小圆；指快物件问 *Is it fast?* 儿童说 *Yes.* 板上圆圈涂红，慢物件同样涂蓝；说 *Fast. Red. Slow. Blue.* 出示页顶颜色键。指照片问 *Fast or slow? Red or blue?* 儿童想想把每张照片旁的圆圈涂红或蓝。
**Track pictures（Learning through play）：** 在托盘里放颜料和小轮玩具车辆（车、摩托、卡车、拖拉机等），确保有不同宽度的轮胎。儿童把车滚进颜料再从左到右「开」过厚纸做轨迹；可用不同车辆、颜色做图案，鼓励他们注意不同车辆轨迹差别，试快慢移动看效果。
**After the book：** 请志愿者举书、指一张照片说 *Fast, red* 或 *Slow, blue.* 然后志愿者描述一张照片让全班猜；问儿童想更多通常慢/快的物件。
**Activity Book（p103）：** *Look. Find. Trace.* 儿童看照片，用蓝色描慢的东西、红色描快的东西；然后举书指点说 *(It's) fast. (It's) slow.*
**Finishing the class：** 播放 **The I can chant（Track 67）**儿童帮忙收拾；宣布下课，用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 104

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** transport.
**Language：** **I can see a (car/van/bike/bus).**
**Materials：** dressing up clothes and props (e.g. police/firefighter helmets, hard hats, tools, yellow tabards, a post bag); 'traffic lights' from Lesson 6; ride-on toys, etc. (optional); Unit 8 flashcards; colouring pencils or crayons (including blue, brown, red and yellow); phone/tablet (optional).
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Creative Thinking: Using newly created content to solve problems and make decisions: Uses own ideas for doing creative activities like colouring, drawing, and building to represent vocabulary and familiar concepts.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；提供玩具和衣物玩「街道」。儿童可扮成警察（散步或假装开警车）、消防员、工程或邮递员；若做过「交通灯」活动的彩色圆，有些儿童可站一处变换圆控制交通。警察说 *Be careful!* 或 *Slow, please!*，交通灯说 *Stop! / Slow! / Go!* 若空间够，儿童也可开 ride-on toys。
**Before the book：** 用闪卡游戏复习车辆（见 TB 13 页）。把车辆闪卡贴板，让儿童说词。用不同颜色圈每张闪卡：bike–blue、van–brown、bus–red、car–yellow。确保所有儿童有蓝、棕、红、黄蜡笔或铅笔。
**With the book（PB p104）*Look. Listen. Find. Circle. Say.*** 播放录音，儿童用正确颜色圈出同类型车辆（如 *I can see four bikes* – 用蓝色圈所有自行车），每句后停核对颜色。*Note：* 本活动没有描线让儿童跟随——他们尝试自己圈，给大量支持和鼓励。再播录音每句后停让儿童重复。儿童轮流举书点名，说一句（如 *I can see four bikes*）并指图里车辆。
**Track 69（录音文本）：** *Girl: I can see four bikes.  Boy: I can see three vans.  Girl: I can see two buses.  Boy: I can see six cars.*
**Activity Book（p104）：** *Look. Draw. Say.* 指示例图，用 L1 说明女孩把自己画在了车辆上。儿童在框里画任想画的（车、自行车、货车或巴士）（本活动没有描线，儿童尝试自己画，给支持和鼓励）。然后给图画涂色，巡视说 *Tell me about your picture.* 个别儿童说名称或 *I can see a (van).* 鼓励也谈颜色；如孩子愿意可给图画标车辆名，并与全班分享，说 *I can see a (bus)* 再揭示图画。可拍摄此活动稍后播放。
**After the book：** 给儿童两分钟看书里的图并记住，说 *Close your book.* 问如 *How many cars? How many girls?* 也可用 L1 问街道设施（如 *What colour is the postbox?*）儿童用英语回答。播录音逐句核对、重复，儿童轮流举书点图说句子。
**Finishing the class：** 再看 Unit 8 引入视频，谈论本单元学到什么；播放 **The transport song（Track 61）**儿童跟上做动作。

---

### Lesson 12（Project 项目课时）—— Pupil's Book page 105

**Objective：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** transport.
**Language：** **I can (ride a bike/run/jump/drive a car).**
**Materials：** paper plates (one per child, pre-cut to look like steering wheels); colouring pencils or crayons; pre-made unit objective picture cards; Activity Book stickers; music for Once I caught a fish alive or pre-made number cards 1–10, hula hoops and beanbags (optional); puppet.
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Learning to Learn: Reflecting on and evaluating own learning success: Self-corrects from own stored knowledge when they realise a mistake has been made.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；教儿童一首数数歌，如 Once I caught a fish alive 的第一段，他们跟上数字并用手指数出。（或在地板/操场画跳房子格子写 1–10 教玩；若不能画地，用带 1–10 数字卡的呼啦圈，儿童尝试往每个数字圈扔豆袋并用英语说数字。）
**Before the book：** 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，让她绕着教室跑，说 *I can run!* 然后跳起说 *I can jump!* Pippa 说含 I can… 的句子，儿童做相应动作。课前如 PB 所示用纸盘做方向盘；本课所有裁剪需提前做好。向全班展示方向盘并假装开它。
**With the book（PB p105）*Look. Make. Play.*** 分发已按图中切好的纸盘，儿童按喜好涂色。巡视评论如 *I like your steering wheel. Well done! Good colouring!*
**Activity Book（p105）：** *Point. Say. Colour.* 儿童指点并说图中儿童在说的话（*I can drive a car. I can run. I can ride a bike. I can jump.*）
**Learning to Learn / After the book：** 举书翻过 Unit 8，说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 8.* 若你用过图卡呈现单元目标，指图谈儿童做过的不同事；问喜欢/不喜欢哪些活动；鼓励儿童唱歌曲、用 L1 复述故事、从 1–10 数数、听指令（如你喊 drive a car 时做动作）。确保儿童手上有方向盘，玩 'Follow my leader'（见 TB 13 页）：儿童在你身后排成队，你用本单元语言下指令，如 *I can jump. I can ride a bike. I can run fast.* 说 *I can drive a car!* 时脱队、手拿一只方向盘在面前开过这片区域；鼓励发引擎音效并喊 *I can drive!* 若儿童犯错，给时间自我纠正；必要时重复错误并用疑问语调停顿等儿童自己改正。
**Self-evaluation：** 按 40 页的自评流程进行；让儿童把 Unit 8 的星级（star）贴纸放好（右上）。
**Finishing the class：** 提醒儿童回家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---

## Unit 9 The Park

单元主题：The park（公园/自然界）。Pupil's Book 页 106–117（原书页 219–241）。

### 单元目标（Unit objectives）

- 熟悉花草动植物词汇：**bird, flower, frog, tree, green, pink**。
- 学会用颜色形容词描述（单数）且谈复数：**A (green) (tree). / They're (butterflies).**
- 认识用复数名词：**butterflies, caterpillars, ladybirds, worms**。
- 学习字母读音 **u**（the letter sound u，以 umbrella 为例）。
- 学习数字概念：adding one more（再加一个）。
- 跨学科（Maths）：**symmetry（对称）**。
- 故事价值：**Look after nature（爱护自然）**；阅读故事 *Penny in the park*。
- 复习与进度评估。

---

### Lesson 1（单元开篇歌曲）—— Pupil's Book page 106

**Objective：** Become familiar with the topic of nature and listen to a song.
**Vocabulary：** bird, flower, frog, tree, green, pink.
**Materials：** toy insects, park birds/animals, trees; pre-made unit objective picture cards; pictures of plants, birds, animals; real leaves, flowers, seed pods or small branches; pictures of the local park[?]；Unit 9 introduction video; flashcards tree, flower, bird, frog, green, pink; red, yellow, blue, brown (Unit 3); Activity Book stickers.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；提供与自然/公园主题相关的玩具，儿童自由选择玩耍；边玩边交谈，如 *Look! A spider! Do you like spiders? Let's count the legs. What colour is this?* 看儿童是否会用英语说出该词。
**Before the book：** 与全班过一遍单元目标（见 TB 19 页，改适 Unit 9）。展示植物、鸟类和动物图片；如可能分发真实树叶、花、籽荚或小树枝供传递。鼓励儿童谈论在哪里能找到这些东西。展示当地公园照片说 *Look! The park!* 问儿童何时去公园、有没有最喜欢的部分。用闪卡复习颜色 red, yellow, blue, brown，并引出 green 和 pink（仅需认识 receptive）。播放 Unit 9 视频引入主题，暂停让儿童谈论看到什么、以为本单元学什么。
**With the book（PB p106）*Listen to the song.*** 指儿童照片说 *Look! I can see a boy and a girl. They're outside. They're looking at things. Plants, flowers, animals. They're looking at nature.* 指青蛙照片，看学生是否会用英语说词。展示闪卡 tree, flower, bird, frog 并说名；儿童在页上找它们。指页角 Pop 和 Pippa 的图，说 *Song time! Listen!* 播放 **The park song** 让儿童听，被提到时举起相关颜色/自然闪卡。再播儿童指点图中相关物件。帮儿童找到 Unit 9 贴纸（tree 和 frog）贴到正确位置。
**Track 70 The park song（录音文本）：** *Pippa: Let's sing about the park!  Pop: Yes! I like the park!  All [chorus]: We're in the park, we're in the park. Yeah! We are in the park. Yeah!  Boy: I can see a green tree [x3]. A green tree in the park.  Girl: I can see a pink flower…  Boy: I can see a blue bird…  Girl: I can see a brown frog…  All [chorus]（每段后重复副歌。）
**After the book：** 再播歌儿童跟上音效；教动作：tree（伸手够天际）、flower（握拳到张开手指）、bird（挥动双臂像翅膀）、frog（跳跃），儿童为每段歌曲做相应动作。*Finishing the class*：用下课常规结束。*Activity Book（p106）：*Listen again. Look. Stick. Point.* 儿童能找到贴纸并跟唱跳舞。

---

### Lesson 2（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 107

**Objective：** Name plants and animals and use colour adjectives.
**Vocabulary：** bird, flower, frog, tree, green, pink.
**Language：** **A (green) (tree).**
**Materials：** flashcards bird, frog, tree, flower, brown, green, pink; red, yellow (Unit 3); puppet; brown, pink, green and blue colouring pencils or crayons; paint; brushes or stamps and plain paper cut into the shapes of leaves and flowers; blue, green and brown display paper.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；教一首颜色歌（用 I can sing a rainbow 曲调）：*I see yellow, brown and green, / Red and pink and blue. / I can sing the colours, / Sing the colours, / Can you sing them, too?*
**Before the book：** 展示闪卡 bird, frog, tree, flower，指说每词，儿童重复。说一个自然词，一名儿童指正确闪卡，重复。发自然闪卡，每个儿童说词再传给下一个人。把闪卡贴板；儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，Pippa 指板说 *I can see a…!* 儿童接说 tree! Pippa 再问 *What colour is the (flower)?*（Pink!）展示花、树、鸟或青蛙图问 *What's this?*（It's a bird.）说如 *Yes! It's a brown bird.* 图在班里传。
**With the book（PB p107）*Listen. Point. Colour.*** 指问 *Who's this?*（Dan, Kim, Daddy/Matt.）说 *They're in the park. What can they see?* 播放音频让儿童听。再播儿童听指点，核对。再播暂停让儿童给四件物品涂色。然后说如 *A green tree.* 儿童指图并重复。
**Track 71（录音文本）：** *Dan: Look, Daddy!  Daddy: Oh, a frog! A brown frog.  Dan: Yes, a brown frog!  Kim: Look, a flower.  Daddy: Yes, a pink flower.  Kim: A pink flower. Oh, I like it.  Dan: Look, a tree.  Kim: A green tree.  Daddy: Yes, a green tree.  Daddy: Look, a bird!  Kim: Oh! A blue bird!  Dan: Yes, I can see a blue bird.*
**Activity Book（p107）：** *Look. Find. Colour. Say.* 提醒儿童用圆点判断该用什么颜色；巡视问如 *What's this?*（A frog.）*What colour is it?*（Brown.）儿童举书指点并说动植物名，如 *It's a blue bird.*
**Learning through play（a colourful tree）：** 提供颜料和树叶/花瓣形状剪纸，每个儿童涂一片叶和一朵花，鼓励混合颜色、用不同工具（手指、刷子、印章、棉球等）画；巡视说如 *I like your yellow flower. What colour do blue and yellow make?* 把叶和花贴到大树的枝干展示上（用彩纸做：绿色地面、蓝色天空、棕色树干树枝）。
**After the book：** 展示闪卡（自然和颜色）指点说词，儿童跟上；播放 **The park song（Track 70）**鼓励跟唱。*Finishing the class*：用 Pippa 做自评，请她问班级反思问题（见 TB 7 页 Learning to Learn 部分）。

---

### Lesson 3（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 108

**Objective：** Practise using colour adjectives.
**Vocabulary：** bird, flower, frog, tree, green, pink.
**Language：** **A (green) (tree).**
**Materials：** flashcards tree, flower, bird, frog; your own photos of wild animals, birds, plants or insects (optional); puppet; Pupil's Book stickers; at least six items in known colours (e.g. a pink ball, a green apple, a blue book, a yellow banana, a brown teddy, a red toy car or train); a tray and a piece of cloth.
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Critical Thinking: Understanding and analysing links between ideas: Sorts, arranges and describes objects by shape, size, colour, weight, texture and position.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The park song（Track 70）**儿童跟上做动作（如手放眼上看、伸手够天、开花、扇翅膀、跳）。
**Before the book：** 展示 tree, flower, bird, frog 闪卡，如可能加真实花/鸟等照片；指说如 *Look! A green frog.* 儿童唤出并问候 Pippa，说明要玩游戏。叫一志愿者上前，Pippa 说如 *I can see a (…?)*，儿童指正确图得分；若错，Pippa 得分，用尽可能多的图/儿童重复。用目标颜色的其他物品墨卡（若无照片），如 *I can see a yellow banana. / Look! A brown teddy.*
**With the book（PB p108）*Listen. Stick. Point. Say.*** 帮儿童找到书后贴纸页；播音频儿童听指正确贴纸；再播暂停贴好。逐指图问 *What's this?*（A brown frog, a blue bird, a pink flower, a green tree.）提醒儿童 Dan 和 Kim 与爸爸在公园辨认动植物；儿童告诉你见过的野生动物。把照片展示出（鸟、蝴蝶或树）。
**Track 72（录音文本）：** *Dan: What's this?  Daddy: It's a pink flower.  Daddy: What can you see?  Kim: I can see a green tree.  Dan: What's this?  Daddy: It's a brown frog.  Daddy: What can you see?  Kim: I can see a blue bird.*
**Activity Book（p108）：** *Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 儿童用指或铅笔圈出每排的「odd one out」（a green tree, a pink flower, a brown frog, a blue bird），巡视问不同儿童 *What's this?* 描完后儿童指点每排 odd one out 并说短语。（备注：OCR 最后一项与前一排重复，以实际图为准，拿不准处标注 `[?]`。）
**After the book：** 在托盘放六件或更多物品，向全班展示问 *What can you see?* 或 *What's this?*（A green apple. A red train.）儿童看图两分钟并记住物品；盖住托盘，儿童回忆（A green apple, etc.）；最后揭示物品核对（*Yes. It's a green apple!*）。（若用无实物，展示六张闪卡并挑战儿童按顺序记住。）描述教室内物件让个别儿童找，如 *It's a blue jacket.*
**At home：** 让儿童在家找粉色和绿色东西，鼓励带一张照片到下次课。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 4（Sounds and letters）—— Pupil's Book page 109

**Objective：** Practise recognising the letter sound u.
**S&L focus：** Practise letter sound u with the word umbrella.
**Materials：** bags with handles (one per pair), each marked with a target colour (pink, red, blue, yellow, brown or green); small items/toys/doll's clothes in these colours; pre-made letter card u; a small umbrella; picture of an umbrella and other items which start with the sound u (e.g. flashcard under (Unit 6), an unhappy face, an uncle, something ugly, an arrow pointing up); pictures of items which don't start with u (e.g. apple, doll, elephant, igloo, bananas, shoe, orange, ostrich).
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Critical Thinking: Understanding and analysing links between ideas: Matches objects, people, letters, pronunciations and words.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；把六种已知颜色的物品（书、玩具、积木、玩具食物、玩偶衣）摆到教室各处。给每对儿童一个标有颜色的袋子；儿童找与袋色匹配的物品放进去。巡视说如 *It's a yellow bird.* 或 *Look under the chair!*
**Before the book：** 在板上写大号小写 'u' 说字母音 u，儿童重复；再指字母让儿童练音。把预制字母卡 u 传开；你说 *Stop and say!* 持卡儿童发音。出示真伞说 umbrella，在字母旁贴伞图，指说 umbrella 发音 u；再指让儿童说词和音。
**With the book（PB p109）*Listen. Trace. Colour. Say.*** *Listen and point!* 播音频儿童指字母或伞图，核实。若班级能描字形，用空中手指从左上开始教如何写 'u'；再用板上字母示范；儿童用指或红蜡笔在页面描字母 u。让儿童把伞涂红以匹配字母。边走动听儿童指字母和伞、发音或说词。
**Track 73（录音文本）：** *u u u — umbrella umbrella umbrella — u umbrella*
**After the book：** *Look and listen: 'u' like 'umbrella' or no?* 告诉儿童听到以 u 开头的词就做举起伞在头上的动作；展示一系列图，有的以 u 开头、有的不是，逐个说词夸大首音，儿童只为 u 开头的做动作。若进度合适，儿童用指尖在彼此背上画字母 u。
**Activity Book（p109）：** *Listen again. Look. Find. Trace. Say.* 再播音频，儿童描字母 u 并圈出以 u 开头的词（伞）。
**Finishing the class：** 儿童展示课程开始时装有物品的颜色袋；轮流取出物品做成短语（如 *A green train*），用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 5（Literacy：故事）—— Pupil's Book pages 110–111

**Objective：** Listen to, enjoy and answer questions about a story.
**Story language：** ladybird, rubbish, look after, **The (flower) is happy (in the park).**
**Materials：** ladybird and flower-themed toys and colouring (optional); pictures of a ladybird and a park with rubbish visible; Big Book; Unit 9 literacy story video.
**Starting the class：** 提供瓢虫和花朵主题玩具/游戏供儿童自由玩（如数红点并匹配瓢虫、「把红点贴在瓢虫上」、花朵配瓢虫的涂色纸）。用讲故事常规安定课堂（见 TB 27 页）。
**Before the book：** 展示瓢虫图说 *It's a ladybird.* 儿童跟读 ladybird；问是否见过瓢虫，解释它们是甲虫、会飞。问儿童上次去公园做了什么；展示有垃圾的公园图说 *Oh no! Rubbish! Rubbish in the park.* 问应该怎么处理垃圾。打开 Big Book 让儿童找标题；请他们指图 1 的 Penny 并猜女人是谁、他们在哪。
**With the book（PB p110–111）*Listen.*** 儿童说出每幅图里发生什么，你用英语转述，如 *Penny wants to pick the yellow flower. Her mummy says 'no'.* 用 L1 解释 look after 的意思。播音频儿童看 Big Book，逐帧指图。每幅后提问：*Where are Penny and her mummy?*（In the park.）*What colour is the flower/ladybird/frog?*（Yellow/red/green.）*Why can't Penny pick the flower?*（Because it's happy in the park.）*Where's the rubbish?*（In the bin.）*Who's happy?*（Penny and the park.）问 *Did Penny take the flower and the ladybird home? What did she do to help the park?* 鼓励儿童用 L1 复述。
**Track 74 —— Penny in the park（录音文本）：** *Penny is in the park.  Penny: Oh, look!  Penny: What's this?  Mummy: It's a flower. A yellow flower.  Penny: I like it!  Mummy: No, Penny! The flower is happy in the park.  Penny: Look. What's this?  Mummy: It's a ladybird. A red ladybird.  Penny: I like it!  Mummy: No, Penny! The ladybird is happy in the park.  Penny: A green frog! Oh. What's this?  Mummy: It's rubbish.  Mummy: Yes, Penny! Let's look after the park.  Mummy: Well done, Penny! Let's go home now.  Penny is happy and the park is happy.*
**After the book：** 用 Big Book 再讲一遍故事，用录音语言、拟声与动作；或播放读写故事视频。*Literacy development tip*：多次讲后儿童会自然地跟上部分语言；鼓励并用受控角色扮演巩固，为重复短语提供图卡提示，在故事停点让儿童说出来。
**Activity Book（p110）：** *Look. Point. Trace.* 指个图说 *Listen and point.* 念故事句子（*Penny's in the park. It's a yellow flower. It's a red ladybird. It's rubbish.*）儿童点正确图。问 *Who is happy in the park? Is Penny happy?*（Yes.）*Is the flower happy?*（Yes.）*Is the ladybird happy?*（Yes.）儿童描圈出 flower、Penny 和 ladybird。
**Finishing the class：** 问 *Did Penny take the flower and the ladybird home? What did she do to help the park?* 儿童用 L1 复述故事。

---

### Lesson 6（Values 价值观）—— Pupil's Book pages 110–111

**Objective：** Recognise the value of looking after nature, from the story.
**Value：** **Look after nature（爱护自然）。**
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Social Responsibilities: Understanding and discussing global issues – environmental, political, financial and social: Describes ways to help the environment (e.g. save water, recycle etc.).
**Story language：** ladybird, rubbish, look after, **The (flower) is happy (in the park).**
**Materials：** flashcards red, pink, yellow, blue; Big Book; colouring pencils or crayons; a picnic blanket, basket or bag and play food, plastic cups, etc. or leaves and bark (optional).
**Starting the class：** 分成四组，各命名：red flowers、pink flowers、yellow flowers、blue flowers；每名组员给相应颜色闪卡。下指令让各组执行，如 *Blue flowers, jump! Yellow flowers, run!* 包含如 *Everyone, be a frog!* 若愿意用 L1 谈回收，可加关于把不同垃圾放进不同垃圾桶（瓶子、纸等）以便再利用的讨论。用讲故事常规安定课堂（见 TB 27 页）。
**Before the book：** 一起观看 Unit 9 读写故事视频，每场景停用 L1 问 *What is happening?* 用 L1 谈论图中儿童如何爱护自然（给瓢虫拍照、不带回家、把垃圾放垃圾桶、种树）。问儿童能如何爱护自己住处的自然（如把蛋留在巢里、不在海滩留垃圾）。问垃圾放哪（放垃圾桶）。再播故事儿童听指。
**With the book（PB p110–111）*Listen.*** 出示 Big Book，请儿童指出 Penny 爱护自然的图（不带花/瓢虫回家、捡垃圾）。用 L1 讨论公园哪些区域可以吃（指定区域）并提醒儿童野餐后收拾好，说 *Let's look after nature.* 问图中每件东西是什么，如 *What's this?*（It's a flower.）*Look! No! The flower's happy in the park. / It's a ladybird. Oh no! The ladybird's happy in the park.*
**Learning through play（a picnic in the park）：** 在一块野餐毯或篮/袋里摆玩具食物；说 *Let's have a picnic in the park!* 鼓励儿童说出食品和饮料（sandwiches, water etc.）并玩耍时用 Please, Thank you, Here you are. 最后收拾好。*Alternatively*：带叶或树皮到课堂用蜡笔做擦印；谈论捡已从树上落下的东西可以，但不摘。
**Activity Book（p111）：** *Look. Find. Colour.* 儿童给爱护自然的每个儿童涂色。
**At home：** 让儿童与家人谈如何爱护自然；可带一张自己做时的照片到课堂（捡垃圾、浇花或种树）。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 7（Language presentation）—— Pupil's Book page 112

**Objective：** Name insects and animals and use plural nouns.
**Vocabulary：** butterflies, caterpillars, ladybirds, worms.
**Language：** **What are these? They're (butterflies).**
**Materials：** plastic insects, tray with leaves or tissue paper, magnifying glasses (optional); insect-related colouring (optional); Unit 9 flashcards; Unit 9 character story video; colouring pencils or crayons (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；把塑料昆虫藏在带叶或纸巾的托盘中，给儿童放大镜，鼓励找所看到的东西；说一些英文词如 *Look! It's a ladybird.*（或摆昆虫/毛毛虫/蠕虫涂色纸让儿童涂色。）巡视用 L1 问是否见过蝴蝶、毛毛虫、蠕虫或瓢虫。
**Before the book：** 展示闪卡 butterflies, caterpillars, ladybirds, worms，教词并练发音（见 TB 13 页），确保儿童正确读出复数词尾。板上画一只蝴蝶说 butterfly；然后指闪卡里的蝴蝶说 butterflies；其他词照做。画一朵花说 *It's a flower.* 画一束花说 *They're flowers.* 给更多例子（展示儿童熟悉实物，如 *It's a book. They're books. It's a shoe. They're shoes.*）
**With the book（PB p112）*Listen. Point. Circle.*** 播放音频让儿童听。*Listen and point.* 再播音频，儿童听指每幅图提到的生物。*Circle.* 说明儿童需圈出成群的生物。念音频里的句子（如 *They're brown worms*），儿童指正确生物。
**Track 75（录音文本）：** *Dan: What are these?  Kim: They're butterflies!  Dan: They're yellow butterflies.  Dan: What are these?  Kim: They're caterpillars!  Dan: They're green caterpillars.  Dan: What are these?  Kim: They're worms!  Dan: They're brown worms.  Kim: Oh, what are these?  Dan: They're red ladybirds. They're my friends.*
**After the book：** 玩 'Pass the card' 用复数物品闪卡（见 TB 13 页）；音乐停时持卡儿童站起说如 *Ladybirds* 或 *They're ladybirds.* 可增加难度发 flower、tree 等闪卡，让儿童判断该用 It's a… 还是 They're… 造句。播放角色故事视频，每场景停用 L1 问 *What's happening?* 解释 They're my friends 的意思。玩 *'Hatching Caterpillars'*：用 L1 解释毛毛虫从蛋里孵化；儿童蹲下装蛋，一起模仿从蛋里钻出来像毛毛虫一样扭动爬行。
**Activity Book（p112）：** *Look. Trace. Say.* 儿童从左侧单个生物照片沿连线到右侧成组照片；巡视鼓励练说如 butterfly, butterflies；描完后儿童举书一起说单复数词（butterfly–butterflies, caterpillar–caterpillars, ladybird–ladybirds, worm–worms）；你说单复数句子，儿童指正确图（*It's a ladybird. They're worms.*）
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 8（Language practice）—— Pupil's Book page 113

**Objective：** Practise They're (butterflies) with a chant.
**Vocabulary：** butterflies, caterpillars, ladybirds, worms.
**Language：** **What are these? They're (butterflies).**
**Materials：** flashcards butterflies, caterpillars, worms, ladybirds; groups of toys, clothes and classroom items (e.g. toy cars, pencils, hats, shoes); Unit 9 chant video; pink, red, blue and green colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；玩 *Make a caterpillar*：儿童排成一行、每人都抱前面人的腰；放音乐儿童转动，队首决定「毛毛虫」怎么动（前进、后退、转身、小步/大步），先由你带领，再换队首。*Alternatively*：让儿童模仿本单元的动植物，如 *Let's be butterflies!*
**Before the book：** 用闪卡复习 butterflies, caterpillars, worms, ladybirds，儿童跟你读写；逐一举卡问 *What are these?* 帮儿童答 *They're…*；拿起教室里成组的物品/玩具问同样问题（如 *What are these?* [They're cars.]）。
**With the book（PB p113）*Listen. Trace. Sing.*** 指图逐个问，如 *Can you see a red butterfly? Can you see a pink worm?* 说明儿童需沿连线匹配生物；播 **The bugs chant** 让儿童听并看图。
**Track 76 The bugs chant（录音文本）：** *(Spread your arms)  Child: Look! What are these?  Children: They're butterflies, they're butterflies. Blue and red butterflies.  Child: Oh, yes! I can see the butterflies.  (Wave 'antennae')  Child: Look! What are these?  Children: They're ladybirds, they're ladybirds. Red and blue ladybirds.  Child: Oh, yes! I can see the ladybirds.  (Wiggle your body)  Child: Look! What are these?  Children: They're worms, they're worms. Pink and brown worms.  Child: Oh, yes! I can see the worms.  (Munch)  Child: Look! What are these?  Children: They're caterpillars, they're caterpillars. Green and yellow caterpillars.  Child: Oh, yes! I can see the caterpillars.*
**Activity Book（p113）：** *Look. Colour. Say.* 提醒儿童核对彩色版图判断黑白图该用什么颜色；巡视问 *What are these? What colour is this? Can you see the butterflies?* 涂完后说句子如 *They're caterpillars.* 儿童指点所涂图的正确部分；然后儿童举书写拼音念 *They're pink worms. They're blue butterflies. They're red ladybirds. They're green caterpillars.*
**After the book：** 再播 chant，儿童做动作并尽量跟词；再播儿童从左到右描线，鼓励跟唱。观看 **The bugs chant** 视频，鼓励儿童跟上。
**At home：** 让儿童在家外找动物（花园或阳台）或附近公园，请家长/看护人拍照带到学校。
**Finishing the class：** 可让儿童用自评技巧表达表演感受（见 TB 7 页 Learning to Learn 部分，当 Pippa 问 Can you do it? 时）；用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 9（Numbers：One more）—— Pupil's Book page 114

**Objective：** Practise numbers 1–10 and adding one more.
**Vocabulary：** numbers 1–10.
**Language：** **One more.**
**Materials：** puppet; a group of ten small items for each pair of children (e.g. toy cars, buttons, pasta shapes); colouring pencils or crayons; a box, if possible decorated to look like a frog (optional); beanbags or balls; a bag.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The bugs chant（Track 76）**儿童跟上做动作。
**Before the book：** 把数字 1–10 写板上并和全班练数数。儿童唤出 Pippa，Pippa 选志愿者从 1–10 大声数。举起两件物品（如两支铅笔）说 *One, two.* 再加一件说 *One more. One, two, three.* 用四支铅笔重复（*One more… five*）和六（six），鼓励儿童跟上数。把教室物品/玩具放前面，叫一志愿者说如 *Three cars.* 儿童取正确数量，再说 *One more!* 儿童再取一件数总数（如 *One, two, three, four cars.*）换数字重复。
**With the book（PB p114）*Listen. Count. Colour.*** 指第一条毛毛虫数它身体的节（one, two, three）。儿童数并完成其余描线，巡视说 *One more!* 再让儿童给右侧三条毛毛虫的最后一节涂色，可边涂边数；涂完再数。*Listen and count.* 播音频儿童指点毛毛虫各部分；再播全班跟上词、边指。
**Track 77（录音文本）：** *1, 2, 3. One more. 1, 2, 3… 4.  1, 2, 3, 4, 5. One more. 1, 2, 3, 4, 5… 6.  1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7. One more. 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7… 8.*
**Activity Book（p114）：** *Look. Count. Trace. Say.* 让儿童数并描第一列火车的车厢；然后数第二列彩色车厢，你数到最后要描的车厢时说 *One more!* 儿童说总数并描车厢；随后儿童举书一起数车厢。
**Learning through play（feed the frog）：** 儿童在豆袋或软球旁排队；放一个（如可能装饰成青蛙的）盒子在适当距离。儿童轮流把豆袋/球扔进盒子（每人可三次）；用英语如 *One, two… One more!* 并计数盒中物品（目标是凑到十）。*Optional song*（用 There were ten in the bed 曲调）：*There's one in the bag and Pippa says 'One more! One more!' / One more in… one, two!* Pippa 边唱边往袋里放玩具（*There are two in the bag and Pippa says 'One more! One more!' … There are ten in the bag and Pippa says 'That's OK!'*）。
**After the book：** 给每对儿童十件小物品；说数字让儿童从堆里数出并移走（如 *Five… One, two, three, four, five. One more! Six!*）。唱上面的一首数数歌（There were ten in the bed）或拍手游戏练一到十。
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 10（Maths：Symmetry）—— Pupil's Book page 115

**Objective：** Talk about symmetry.
**Vocabulary：** same.
**Language：** **Are they the same?**
**Materials：** colouring pencils or crayons; pictures of symmetrical things (e.g. glasses, a window, a slice of bread, a black ball) and asymmetrical things (e.g. a fish, a hand or foot, a tree), all cut in half; red, blue, pink, yellow, brown and black colouring pencils or crayons; pictures of things showing symmetry in nature; red paper plates; black paper circles or stickers; black pipe cleaners or curls of black paper; glue.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；播放 **The bugs chant（Track 76）**儿童跟上做动作。
**Before the book：** 举起一张对称物体图的两半（如一副眼镜）问 *Are they the same?*（先 L1 再英语。）（Yes.）用对称物体/生物图重复。再展示不对称物体的两半（如手）问是否一样（No.），用更多不对称图重复。随机出示分裂图（有对称有不对称），每次问 *Are they the same?* 儿童可说 Yes/No 或用动作表示。把「Before the book」图的两半混在一起让儿童分类匹配；巡视指两半问 *Are they the same?* 儿童说已知物品名并谈论颜色。
**With the book（PB p115）*Look. Colour.*** 板上画心形的一边、另一边靠得很近但没连上；指两半问 *Are they the same?* 儿童说 *Yes.* 在图右下画个小圆并涂红，说 *Red for the same.* 板上面半支铅笔（尖的那部分）再画另一半但不相连，问 *Are they the same?* 儿童说 *No.* 摇头，把图右下的小圆圆蓝，用 L1 解释红色表示两半相同、蓝色表示两半不同。儿童想想把显示对称的图（蝴蝶和叶）旁边的圆涂红，其余圆（岩石和树）涂蓝，巡视帮助。儿童举书核对答案。
**Learning through play（make a ladybird）：** 儿童用红纸盘做瓢虫；在每盘中央画黑色线。儿童贴上黑毛根/黑纸卷当触角，用贴纸做黑点图案；有些儿童可能选择做对称图案。巡视指盘的两半问 *Are they the same?*（可把瓢虫添加到 Lesson 2 的树展示上。）
**After the book：** 展示更多自然中的对称图（叶和花、海星、贝壳、水果切片如橙子、猕猴桃、蜂巢）；问儿童想其他看起来对称的昆虫（蜘蛛、甲虫）。
**Activity Book（p115）：** *Look. Trace. Colour. Say.* 儿童描画并涂色使其对称；鼓励指点并说物件和颜色（*A pink flower. A brown hat. A blue car. A red apple.*）
**Finishing the class：** 用下课常规结束。

---

### Lesson 11（Review）—— Pupil's Book page 116

**Objective：** Review learning and progress in the unit.
**Vocabulary：** the park.
**Language：** **a pink worm, a green frog, a yellow caterpillar, a blue bird.**
**Materials：** toys for playing 'parks' (magnifying glasses, plastic insects, soft toy birds and frogs, peaked caps, softballs); Unit 9 flashcards; colouring pencils or crayons (including pink, green, yellow and blue).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；提供玩具玩「公园」。有些儿童玩跳房子或软球接传，有些模仿骑自行车或滑板车；其他儿童戴放大镜四处走、找昆虫和植物。如可能为两三名儿童提供统一式样的帽子当公园管理员，照顾公园。边看边评论，鼓励儿童尽可能用英语（跳房子的可以数一到十；看自然的说如 *I can see a bird. Stop! Look! No rubbish!*；公园管理员说）。
**Before the book：** 用闪卡复习 Unit 9 词汇，如玩 'Flashcard reveal'（见 TB 13 页）；鼓励儿童谈到单个物件的颜色（a green frog, etc.）。做迷宫示范：指入口处的粉蜡笔问 *What colour?*（Pink.）拿起粉色蜡笔沿路慢慢走直到蜗牛，一路说 *Pink… pink… pink… a pink worm!* 示范给蜗牛涂色并说 *A pink worm.* 确保所有儿童有粉、绿、黄、蓝蜡笔或铅笔。儿童走迷宫时说颜色和生物词，巡视鼓励做短语（如 a yellow caterpillar）。儿童轮流举书说一个短语（如 a pink worm）并指正确图；或结对指点。
**With the book（PB p116）*Follow. Colour. Say.*** 儿童完成后给图涂色，巡视说 *Tell me about your picture.* 个别儿童说出名称/颜色或 *I can see a (blue) (bird).* 如孩子愿意可给图画标注。图完成后问如 *Is there a green tree in your picture? Is there a brown frog? Is there a red flower?* 儿童看图，能答 Yes 就站起、向全班展示图中那部分、如可能重复短语（如 a brown frog）。评估时可把此活动视频拍下稍后播放。
**After the book：** 在板上逐笔慢慢画一株植物或生物；停下让儿童猜是什么，如 *A ladybird!* 或 *It's a ladybird!* 可做成竞赛（四次猜不中你得一分）。
**Finishing the class：** 再看 Unit 9 引入视频，谈论儿童看到什么、本单元学到什么；播放 **The park song（Track 70）**儿童跟上做动作。

---

### Lesson 12（Project 项目课时）—— Pupil's Book page 117

**Objective：** Do a craft activity to review unit language and review progress; carry out self-evaluation.
**Vocabulary：** the park.
**Language：** **They're (butterflies).**
**Materials：** photocopiable butterfly template, page 255 (one per child); paints; scissors; unit objective picture cards; colouring pencils or crayons; Activity Book stickers; animation/video of the life stages of a butterfly (optional); pictures of butterflies and ladybirds.
**Skills（Cambridge Life Competencies）：** Learning to Learn: Reflecting on and evaluating own learning success: Self-corrects from own stored knowledge when they realise a mistake has been made.
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；确保儿童有足够空间，用 L1 解释他们要当蛋、毛毛虫再到蝴蝶；如可能播放蝴蝶生命阶段动画/视频。示范动作让儿童模仿：*We are eggs.* 蜷成球，慢慢模仿伸展并破壳而出；*Caterpillars!* 像毛毛虫一样扭动、模仿咀嚼变大；最后挥臂站起「飞」走说 *Butterflies!*（此时可放一首蝴蝶歌曲。）
**Before the book：** 展示蝴蝶和瓢虫图，问颜色和图案；问 *Are they the same?* 指出对称。课前复印/打印并剪出蝴蝶模板，做一两幅对称画（如 PB 所示），你可为班级剪好形状。展示做好的蝴蝶示例。
**With the book（PB p117）*Look. Make. Say.*** 儿童剪出蝴蝶（给足帮助）；儿童只涂一半，然后折叠模板做出对称图。巡视评论如 *A blue and yellow butterfly. Well done! I like your red and brown butterfly.* 涂完后儿童可随意用黑卡或毛根做触角粘到蝴蝶上。向伙伴或小组展示自己的蝴蝶，说如 *My butterfly is green, blue and brown.*
**Activity Book（p117）：** *Point. Say. Colour.* 儿童指点说 *They're butterflies. They're ladybirds. They're worms. They're caterpillars.*
**After the book：** 干后把蝴蝶加到 Lesson 2 的树展示（或现在用儿童帮忙做展示——见 TB 220 页）；指展示不同元素问 *What's this? / What are these?* 儿童说如 *It's a brown tree. They're blue flowers. It's a butterfly! They're ladybirds.*
**Learning to Learn / Self-evaluation：** 举书翻过 Unit 9，说 *Look! Wow! We've finished Unit 9.* 若你用图卡呈现过单元目标，指图谈儿童做过的不同事；问喜欢/不喜欢哪些活动；鼓励儿童唱歌曲、用 L1 复述故事、点数并加一、用名词前形容词谈颜色（如 *It's a red and brown butterfly.*）。练习用英语数（two butterflies, three ladybirds, four worms, three caterpillars）；若犯错给时间自我纠正。按 40 页自评流程进行；儿童把 Unit 9 星级（star）贴纸放好（右上）。
**Finishing the class：** 提醒儿童回家练习；唱 **The goodbye song（Track 2）**结束。

---
## Units 7–9 Review

复习页：Pupil's Book pages 118–119（原书页 243–245），分两课（Language review、Language and numbers review）。

### Review Lesson 1 —— Language review（PB p118）

**Objective：** Review language from Units 7–9.
**Vocabulary：** clothes, colours, the park, transport, verbs.
**Language：** **I can see a bus. I can (run). Put on / Take off (your jacket). I've got (trousers). It's a (green) (tree). They're (butterflies).**
**Materials：** puppet; flashcards from Units 7–9; photos of nature (trees, flowers, frogs, worms, etc.); green, yellow, blue, red and pink colouring pencils or crayons.
**Starting the class：** 播 **The clothes song（Track 53）**，儿童与 Pippa 一起边唱边做动作。出示照片让儿童说 *It's (a green frog)* 或 *They're (brown worms)*；再播录音最后部分让儿童指说。
**Before the book：** 把 Units 7–9 的混合闪卡放板上；选一件物品说如 *Clothes! T-shirt! I've got a red T-shirt.* 把 T 恤卡放一侧；请不同儿童以同样方式找出其他衣物闪卡放在一起，尽量多说关于物品的话；如此把闪卡分类为三类：clothes；transport and verbs；plants, animals and insects。用闪卡游戏（TB 13 页）复习交通。
**With the book（PB p118）*Listen. Find. Point. Say.*** 播音频第一段，儿童听指图中交通（暂停让其说句子）。为儿童说句子让其模仿（如 *I can run*）玩「Simon says」游戏；再播第二段，儿童指认图中人物。*Listen, find and point.*（Track 78。）
**Track 78（录音文本）：** *Look at the boys and girls. Listen and point. Point to the boy. Boy: I can run! I can run! Point to the girl. Girl: I can jump! I can jump! Point to the boy. Boy: I can ride a bike! I can ride a bike! Look at the mummy and children. Listen and point. Woman: Your jacket! Take off your jacket. Point to the jacket. Woman: Your hat! Put on your hat. Point to the hat. Boy: Trousers. I've got trousers. Point to the trousers. Can you see a green tree? Point to a green tree. Say 'It's a green tree.' Can you see a red flower? Point to a red flower. Say 'It's a red flower.' Can you see a blue bird? Point to a blue bird. Say 'It's a blue bird.' Can you see pink butterflies? Point to the butterflies. Say 'They're butterflies.' Can you see the bus? Point to the bus. Say 'I can see a bus.' Can you see the car? Point to the car. Say 'I can see a car.' Can you see the van? Point to the van. Say 'I can see a van.' Can you see the bike? Point to the bike. Say 'I can see a bike'.*
**After the book：** 数图中物品，如 *Let's count the caterpillars.*
**Activity Book（p118）：** *Look. Find. Colour. Say.* 指页底 key 问 *Can you see a (caterpillar)? / Can you see (socks)? What colour is it / are they?* 儿童在大图找东西，用同色涂色；涂后结对指说，如 *It's a green caterpillar. They're blue socks.*
**Finishing the class：** 播放 Units 7–9 中儿童最喜欢的歌曲，跟上做动作。

### Review Lesson 2 —— Language and numbers review（PB p119）

**Objective：** Review language from Units 7–9 and counting.
**Vocabulary：** clothes, colours, numbers 1–10, the park, transport, verbs.
**Language：** **(Four) (pink) (worms).**
**Materials：** whistle (optional); puppet; toy vehicles, toy insects, etc.; pink, blue, red, yellow and green colouring pencils or crayons; pre-made number cards 1–10 and pegs or hula hoops and beanbags (optional).
**Starting the class：** 用开课常规安定课堂；用拍手游戏或数字歌复习数字 1–10（如 *Once I caught a fish alive* 第一段）。在板上写数字 1–10，指数字让儿童说（先按序再随机）。请 Pippa 恭喜全班完成整学年并做得很好；儿童叫 Pippa 并问候。
**Before the book：** 说 *Boys and girls, move around…!* 儿童在教室活动（或更大空间跑动），吹哨即停；说如 *Put on your hat!* 儿童照做直到再次吹哨；用不同指令重复，逐个示范动作（Ride a bike. Run! Jump like a frog. Drive a car. Be a butterfly. Drive a train. Be a tree.）；可大喊 *Fast!* 或 *Slow!* 改变速度以保持活跃。在教室前摆玩具车、蜡笔、铅笔、塑料昆虫等；请儿童上前把同类物品放成一组、用英语数并指板上正确数字；Pippa 帮忙数并说 *One more!*
**With the book（PB p119）*Look. Count. Trace. Say.*** 指数字让儿童说词（eight, nine, ten）；举书说 *How many T-shirts?* 指 T 恤并数出，鼓励全班跟数（*One, two… eight, nine!*）；请儿童指与 T 恤数匹配的数字。示范用绿笔描数字 8、再描 8 与 T 恤之间连线的绿色部分；同样用粉、黄笔描车与帽子。结对练习谈说（如 *Nine cars.*）。
**Activity Book（p119）：** *Look. Count. Colour.* 确保儿童有粉、蓝、红、黄蜡笔；他们看图数每组生物或花朵，并把与数量匹配的圈涂色、把生物/花朵与圈配套；先一起做第一个示例。示范用绿铅笔描数字 8。
**After the book：** 叫个别儿童上前选一张预制数字卡、数出正确数量夹子装到卡上；或给结对儿童两三张数字卡和夹子让他们自己完成。*Alternatively*：地面放八个呼啦圈各放一张数字卡；志愿者站在约一米外，喊一个数字，儿童把豆袋扔进正确圈，轮流进行。
**Finishing the class：** 播放书中儿童最喜欢的游戏；用「Say Goodbye, Pippa」常规结束课堂（TB 17 页）。

## Project Photocopiable Templates

以下为教师用书末尾（原书页 246–256）的**可复印剪贴/手工模板**（PHOTOCOPIABLE，© Cambridge University Press 2022）清单。各模板内容为裁剪图（OCR 无法识别具体图案，仅记录对应单元与项目课时），供备课打印使用。

| 原书页 | 对应项目 | 项目课时书页 | 用途（剪裁/手工） |
|------|---------|------------|------------------|
| 246–247 | Unit 1 project | PB p17 | 剪贴模板（两张） |
| 248 | Unit 2 project | PB p29 | 剪裁模板 |
| 249 | Unit 3 project | PB p41 | 剪裁模板 |
| 250–251 | Unit 5 project | PB p67 | 剪贴模板（两张） |
| 252 | Unit 6 project | PB p79 | 剪裁模板（Cat 猫 / Dog 狗） |
| 253–254 | Unit 7 project | PB p93 | 剪裁模板（两张） |
| 255–256 | Unit 9 project | PB p117 | 蝴蝶剪贴模板（两张；Unit 9 Lesson 12 用页 255） |

说明：
- 各页均标注 `✁`/`✃`（沿虚线裁剪）图标，提示裁剪位置。
- 注：Unit 4 与 Unit 8 无对应复印模板（其项目使用日常材料，未收录在此）。
- 模板图案需对照 Pupil's Book 相应项目页使用。
